📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期登革病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • Ad5-ZIKV-E mut免疫小鼠诱导的JEV交叉反应性或DENV交叉反应性抗体水平低于Ad5-ZIKV-E WT免疫小鼠。Ad5-ZIKV-E mut诱导的抗体在体外增强JEV或DENV感染的能力也较Ad5-ZIKV-E WT有所减弱。
  • 本研究确定了与登革病毒感染相关的周围神经系统并发症的范围,包括单神经病、多发性神经病和吉兰-巴雷综合征,这些并发症在暴发期间的患者中被观察到。 [10.1177/00494755261506378]。
  • 在患有眼科异常和任何临床或影像学异常的妊娠中,观察到新生儿抗DENV IgG反应较低,且这两种关联在假发现率调整后仍保持显著。 [10.1111/1348-0421.70105]。
  • 两种格式均能扩增所有六种ivRNA标准品,且无交叉扩增,其配对的双靶-多靶Ct差异均小于±1.41。对于最近流行的菌株标准品(AF_2020和MY_2024),两种格式的分析灵敏度相当(LoD95分别约为211.7和8.3拷贝/反应)。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • The levels of JEV-cross-reactive or DENV-cross-reactive antibody responses induced by Ad5-ZIKV-E mut immunization in mice are lower than those induced by Ad5-ZIKV-E WT. [10.1128/spectrum.01620-26].
  • The study identified a range of peripheral neurological complications associated with dengue virus infection, including mononeuropathy, polyneuropathy, and Guillain-Barré syndrome, observed in patients during the outbreak period.
  • Lower neonatal anti-DENV IgG reactivity was observed in pregnancies with ophthalmological abnormalities and with any clinical or imaging abnormality, and both associations remained significant after false discovery rate adjustment.
  • Both formats amplified all six ivRNA standards without cross-amplification, with paired dual–multiplex Ct differences below ± 1.41.

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 本期登革病毒新闻集中呈现以下动态。
  • 报告了一起登革热暴发疫情,疑似病例定义为急性发热性疾病持续超过2天并符合特定临床标准的个体,确诊病例则需要通过实验室检测登革病毒或特异性抗原/抗体来确认。 [news-fc2a5127c35cb2fe]。
  • 登革病毒暴发在马绍尔群岛、萨摩亚和瓦努阿图被报告。马绍尔群岛在615份检测样本中发现了173例阳性病例(确认和可能病例),其中最后确认的病例在马朱罗岛发病时间为10月18日。在瓦努阿图,从EpiWeek 41到45期间报告了259例新的实验室确认病例,暴发仍在持续并受到密切监测。报告还提到了澳大利亚的一例麻疹病例和瓦努阿图的一例百日咳暴发,但这些并非主要关注点。
  • 2026年第42流行病学周,美洲地区共报告了1,914,591例疑似登革热病例,由PAHO发布。 [news-4990514e5bc30757]。
  • 一名登革热患者病情危重,出现了严重并发症,包括血小板减少、肝损伤和凝血障碍。国家热带病医院的医生警告称,该疾病可能表现出病情恶化的迹象,并存在重症登革热出血热的风险。 [news-88ed467cd3ea4fe6]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following Dengue virus news developments.
  • A dengue outbreak was reported, with suspected cases defined by acute fever illness lasting more than 2 days and meeting specific clinical criteria, and confirmed cases requiring laboratory testing for dengue virus or specific antigens/antibodies.
  • Dengue outbreaks were reported in the Marshall Islands, Samoa, and Vanuatu.
  • A total of 1,914,591 suspected dengue cases were reported in the Region of the Americas during epidemiological week 42, 2026, by PAHO. [news-4990514e5bc30757].
  • A dengue fever patient in critical condition was reported, with severe complications including low platelet counts, liver damage, and coagulation disorders.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Tropical Doctor  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-16  |  DOI: 10.1177/00494755261506378  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 494755261506378

与登革病毒感染相关的周围神经系统并发症:回顾性病例系列研究

Peripheral neurological complications associated with dengue virus infection: A retrospective case series
作者： Francisco Greloni, Federico Mackinnon, Francisco José Varela
摘要中文翻译登革热的神经系统并发症仍未被完全阐明。我们回顾性描述了在阿根廷2023-2024年暴发期间,在三级神经科转诊中心接受评估的成年患者中,与实验室支持或临床疑似登革热暂时相关的周围神经系统综合征。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热的神经系统并发症仍未被完全阐明。本研究旨在描述在阿根廷暴发期间,与登革病毒感染相关周围神经系统综合征的临床特征。
设计与对象
我们回顾性描述了在阿根廷2023-2024年暴发期间,在三级神经科转诊中心接受评估的成年患者中,与实验室支持或临床疑似登革热暂时相关的周围神经系统综合征。
核心方法
本研究涉及对在阿根廷2023-2024年暴发期间,在三级神经科转诊中心接受评估的成年患者中,与实验室支持或临床疑似登革热暂时相关的周围神经系统综合征的回顾性病例系列分析。
主要结果
本研究确定了与登革病毒感染相关的周围神经系统并发症的范围,包括单神经病、多发性神经病和吉兰-巴雷综合征,这些并发症在暴发期间的患者中被观察到。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为突显登革热感染中被低估的周围神经系统并发症谱系,提供了对该疾病临床表现的新见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为登革热患者的神经系统并发症提供监测和诊断信息,强调在受影响人群中提高临床意识的必要性。
局限与证据强度
本研究受到回顾性设计和回忆偏倚的限制,但由于详细的临床描述和与登革热的时间关联,其证据是强有力的。

Peripheral neurological complications associated with dengue virus infection: A retrospective case series

Authors: Francisco Greloni, Federico Mackinnon, Francisco José Varela
Original abstract (English)
Neurological complications of dengue remain incompletely characterized. We retrospectively describe adults evaluated at a tertiary neurological referral centre during Argentina's 2023-2024 outbreak with peripheral neurological syndromes temporally associated with laboratory-supported or clinically suspected dengue.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Neurological complications of dengue remain incompletely characterized. This study aimed to describe the clinical features of peripheral neurological syndromes associated with dengue virus infection during an outbreak in Argentina.
Design and population
We retrospectively describe adults evaluated at a tertiary neurological referral centre during Argentina's 2023-2024 outbreak with peripheral neurological syndromes temporally associated with laboratory-supported or clinically suspected dengue.
Core methods
The study involved a retrospective case series of adults with peripheral neurological syndromes evaluated at a tertiary neurological referral centre during Argentina's 2023-2024 outbreak, with cases temporally associated with laboratory-supported or clinically suspected dengue.
Main results
The study identified a range of peripheral neurological complications associated with dengue virus infection, including mononeuropathy, polyneuropathy, and Guillain-Barré syndrome, observed in patients during the outbreak period.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as highlighting the under-recognized spectrum of peripheral neurological complications in dengue infection, offering novel insights into the clinical manifestations of the disease.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings can inform the surveillance and diagnosis of neurological complications in dengue patients, emphasizing the need for heightened clinical awareness in affected populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential for recall bias, but the evidence is strong due to the detailed clinical descriptions and temporal association with dengue.

一种结合分子策略用于西非和亚洲森林传播登革病毒 2(DENV-2/GVI)的稳健检测和快速分支分类

A combined molecular strategy for robust detection and rapid clade classification of sylvatic dengue virus 2 (DENV-2/GVI)
作者： Agathe Shella Efire, Ndeye Aminata Dia, Mignane Ndiaye, Manfred Weidmann, Oumar Faye, Idrissa Dieng
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景。西非和亚洲森林传播的登革病毒 2(DENV-2/GVI)基因型在非人灵长类动物和森林蚊虫之间循环,但已有文献报道在塞内加尔和马来西亚出现人类跨物种溢出感染。这些菌株常被商业检测方法误判为"未确定血清型",而一种先前验证的RT-qPCR方法仅能检测非洲分支,无法检测马来西亚分支。利用一组精心挑选的25个完整或近完整的DENV-2/GVI 基因组,我们识别了E基因(非洲分支)和NS5基因(马来西亚分支)中保守但具有分支特异性的区域,并设计了两种格式的寡核苷酸探针:一种用于分组检测(所有探针均标记为FAM),另一种用于同时进行差异检测和分类(非洲探针标记为Cy5,马来西亚探针标记为FAM)。使用覆盖两个分支和广泛时间范围的六种体外转录RNA(ivRNA)标准品进行分析验证。两种格式均能扩增所有六种ivRNA标准品,且无交叉扩增,其配对的双靶-多靶Ct差异均小于±1.41。对于最近流行的菌株标准品(AF_2020和MY_2024),两种格式的分析灵敏度相当(LoD95分别约为211.7和8.3拷贝/反应)。MY_2008标准品显示出格式依赖性的差异(LoD分别为6.9和64.2拷贝/反应),这一差异仍需进一步解决。未观察到与城市型DENV-1至DENV-4之间的交叉反应。使用非洲样本进行的临床验证显示两种格式的结果相当。这种结合策略使单次反应即可实现对非洲和马来西亚分支的西非DENV-2的分子检测扩展,从而实现快速检测和谱系分类,无需系统性基因组测序。本研究主要为分析性研究;尽管已在非洲分支的自然分离株和临床样本上进行了验证,但建议进一步扩展至马来西亚分支的对应样本。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在解决检测和分类西非和亚洲森林传播的登革病毒 2(DENV-2/GVI)菌株的局限性,这些菌株常被商业检测方法误判为"未确定血清型",且无法被现有的RT-qPCR方法检测到。
设计与对象
本研究涉及使用一组精心挑选的25个完整或近完整的DENV-2/GVI 基因组开发和验证分子检测策略,并使用覆盖两个分支和广泛时间范围的六种体外转录RNA(ivRNA)标准品进行分析验证。
核心方法
研究人员识别了DENV-2/GVI的E基因(非洲分支)和NS5基因(马来西亚分支)中保守但具有分支特异性的区域,并分别设计了用于分组检测的双靶格式和用于差异检测与分类的多靶格式寡核苷酸探针。
主要结果
两种格式均能扩增所有六种ivRNA标准品,且无交叉扩增,其配对的双靶-多靶Ct差异均小于±1.41。对于最近流行的菌株标准品(AF_2020和MY_2024),两种格式的分析灵敏度相当(LoD95分别约为211.7和8.3拷贝/反应)。
解释与创新
本研究提出了一种新颖的结合分子策略,能够在无需系统性基因组测序的情况下,实现对西非DENV-2的快速检测和谱系分类,填补了当前诊断方法的空白。
科研与公卫意义
该策略可以提高西非和马来西亚分支的西非DENV-2在血清学和血清流行学方面的检测能力,有助于更准确地识别和分类循环菌株。
局限与证据强度
本研究主要为分析性研究,缺乏大规模临床验证,尽管使用非洲样本在两种格式中均显示出相当的结果。由于验证范围有限,证据强度中等。

A combined molecular strategy for robust detection and rapid clade classification of sylvatic dengue virus 2 (DENV-2/GVI)

Authors: Agathe Shella Efire, Ndeye Aminata Dia, Mignane Ndiaye, Manfred Weidmann, Oumar Faye, Idrissa Dieng
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background. The sylvatic genotype of dengue virus 2 (DENV-2/GVI) circulates between non-human primates and forest mosquitoes in West Africa and Asia, but spillover infections to humans have been documented in Senegal and Malaysia. These strains are frequently reported as "undetermined serotype" by commercial assays, and a previously validated RT-qPCR covers only the African clade, leaving the divergent Malaysian clade undetected. Using a curated set of 25 complete or near-complete DENV-2/GVI genomes, we identified conserved yet clade-discriminatory regions in the E (African clade) and NS5 (Malaysian clade) genes and designed oligonucleotides in two formats: a dual-target format for grouped detection (all probes FAM-labelled) and a multiplex format for simultaneous differential detection and classification (African probe Cy5, Malaysian probe FAM). Six in vitro-transcribed RNA (ivRNA) standards spanning both clades and a wide temporal range were used for analytical validation. Both formats amplified all six ivRNA standards without cross-amplification, with paired dual–multiplex Ct differences below ± 1.41. For the most recent circulating standards (AF_2020 and MY_2024), analytical sensitivity was comparable between formats (LoD95 ≈ 211.7 and 8.3 copies/reaction, respectively). The MY_2008 standard showed a format-dependent difference (LoD 6.9 vs 64.2 copies/reaction) that remains to be resolved. No cross-reactivity was observed against urban DENV-1–4. Clinical validation using African samples shows comparable results in both formats. This combined strategy extends molecular detection of sylvatic DENV-2 to both the African and Malaysian clades in a single reaction, enabling rapid detection and lineage classification without systematic genome sequencing. The present work is mainly analytical; despite validation on African clade natural isolates and clinical samples expansion to Malaysian Clade counterpart samples is encouraged.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the limitations in detecting and classifying sylvatic dengue virus 2 (DENV-2/GVI) strains, which are frequently misclassified as "undetermined serotype" by commercial assays and are not detected by existing RT-qPCR methods.
Design and population
The study involved the development and validation of molecular detection strategies using a curated set of 25 complete or near-complete DENV-2/GVI genomes, with analytical validation using six in vitro-transcribed RNA standards spanning both clades and a wide temporal range.
Core methods
The researchers identified conserved yet clade-discriminatory regions in the E and NS5 genes of DENV-2/GVI and designed dual-target and multiplex oligonucleotides for grouped detection and differential classification, respectively.
Main results
Both formats amplified all six ivRNA standards without cross-amplification, with paired dual–multiplex Ct differences below ± 1.41. For the most recent circulating standards (AF_2020 and MY_2024), analytical sensitivity was comparable between formats (LoD95 ≈ 211.7 and 8.3 copies/reaction, respectively).
Interpretation and novelty
The study presents a novel combined molecular strategy that enables rapid detection and lineage classification of sylvatic DENV-2 without systematic genome sequencing, addressing the gap in current diagnostic methods.
Scientific and public-health significance
This strategy can improve surveillance and diagnosis of sylvatic DENV-2 in both African and Malaysian clades, facilitating more accurate identification and classification of circulating strains.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is mainly analytical and lacks clinical validation on a broader scale, though it shows comparable results in both formats using African samples. The evidence is moderate due to the limited scope of validation.

基于计算的登革病毒 NS1诱导的内皮高通透性的抑制剂astragaloside II的鉴定与体外评估

Computational identification and in vitro evaluation of astragaloside II as an inhibitor of dengue virus NS1-induced endothelial hyperpermeability
作者： Chanati Jantrachotechatchawan, Wanida Chawnawa, Natthawadee Leephatarakit, Warun Maneepitasut, Sarunya Maneerattanasak, Pongpak Manoret, Koravit Poysungnoen, Chompunuch Boonarkart, Chunya Puttikhunt, Panisadee Avirutnan
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 登革热是一种由蚊子传播的病毒性疾病,严重病例的特点是血浆从血管中渗漏。登革病毒分泌的非结构蛋白1(NS1)会破坏内皮屏障,是药物靶点,但目前尚无批准的抗病毒药物。我们旨在鉴定针对NS1的化合物,并测试所选化合物astragaloside II(ASII)是否能对抗NS1诱导的内皮高通透性。方法/主要发现 我们构建了一个深度学习模型,该模型通过化学指纹和蛋白质序列预测化合物与靶点的结合,并利用该模型对四种登革病毒血清型的NS1进行预筛选,共筛选了177种天然化合物。该模型在BindingDB保留数据集和两个独立测试集上均优于DeepConv-DTI基线模型,在召回率和F1分数方面表现更优。ASII被预测能够结合NS1,并被对接到NS1二聚体上。在人类微血管内皮细胞(HMEC-1)中,NS1将相对跨内皮电阻力降低至0.56(P < 0.05)。结论/意义 ASII是进一步研究NS1诱导的内皮高通透性调节剂的候选化合物。其作用机制尚未明确,需要进一步研究。此处未测试其直接与NS1结合、在感染细胞中的活性及其他内皮模型中的效应,计算预测结果需要实验验证。作者摘要 登革热是一种由蚊子传播的病毒性疾病,每年感染数百万人。在严重病例中,液体从最小的血管中渗漏,可能导致休克和死亡。目前尚无批准的药物用于治疗感染本身。一种名为NS1的病毒蛋白,被感染细胞释放到血液中,通过破坏血管内壁导致这种渗漏。因此,我们寻找一种能够阻断NS1有害作用的化合物。我们训练了一个计算机程序,预测哪些化学物质可能与特定蛋白质结合,并利用该程序对177种天然化合物进行排名。我们选择了排名最高的化合物之一astragaloside II,它来源于一种用于传统中医的植物。在血管细胞培养中,NS1削弱了细胞层,而astragaloside II则防止了这种损伤,且不会伤害细胞。该化合物还减少了NS1与细胞表面类似糖的分子的结合,但在保护细胞的剂量下仅表现出较弱的抑制作用。我们未在感染细胞或动物中测试该化合物,因此将其视为进一步研究的先导化合物,而非治疗药物。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热是一种由蚊子传播的病毒性疾病,严重病例的特点是血浆从血管中渗漏。登革病毒分泌的非结构蛋白1(NS1)会破坏内皮屏障,是药物靶点,但目前尚无批准的抗病毒药物。
设计与对象
我们旨在鉴定针对NS1的化合物,并测试所选化合物astragaloside II(ASII)是否能对抗NS1诱导的内皮高通透性。我们训练了一个计算机程序,预测哪些化学物质可能与特定蛋白质结合,并利用该程序对177种天然化合物进行排名。
核心方法
我们构建了一个深度学习模型,该模型通过化学指纹和蛋白质序列预测化合物与靶点的结合,并利用该模型对四种登革病毒血清型的NS1进行预筛选,共筛选了177种天然化合物。我们选择了排名最高的化合物之一astragaloside II,它来源于一种用于传统中医的植物。
主要结果
在血管细胞培养中,NS1削弱了细胞层,而astragaloside II则防止了这种损伤,且不会伤害细胞。该化合物还减少了NS1与细胞表面类似糖的分子的结合,但在保护细胞的剂量下仅表现出较弱的抑制作用。
解释与创新
该模型在BindingDB保留数据集和两个独立测试集上均优于DeepConv-DTI基线模型,在召回率和F1分数方面表现更优。ASII被预测能够结合NS1,并被对接到NS1二聚体上。
科研与公卫意义
ASII是进一步研究NS1诱导的内皮高通透性调节剂的候选化合物。我们未在感染细胞或动物中测试该化合物,因此将其视为进一步研究的先导化合物,而非治疗药物。
局限与证据强度
此处未测试其直接与NS1结合、在感染细胞中的活性及其他内皮模型中的效应,计算预测结果需要实验验证。

Computational identification and in vitro evaluation of astragaloside II as an inhibitor of dengue virus NS1-induced endothelial hyperpermeability

Authors: Chanati Jantrachotechatchawan, Wanida Chawnawa, Natthawadee Leephatarakit, Warun Maneepitasut, Sarunya Maneerattanasak, Pongpak Manoret, Koravit Poysungnoen, Chompunuch Boonarkart, Chunya Puttikhunt, Panisadee Avirutnan
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Dengue is a mosquito-borne viral disease in which severe cases are marked by leakage of plasma from blood vessels. The secreted nonstructural protein 1 (NS1) of dengue virus damages the endothelial barrier and is a drug target, but no approved antiviral exists. We aimed to identify compounds that target NS1 and to test whether the selected compound, astragaloside II (ASII), counteracts NS1-induced endothelial hyperpermeability. Methodology/Principal findings We built a deep learning model that predicts compound– target binding from chemical fingerprints and protein sequences, and used it to prescreen 177 natural compounds against NS1 of the four dengue virus serotypes. The model outperformed the DeepConv-DTI baseline in recall and F1-score on a BindingDB holdout set and on two independent test sets. ASII was predicted to bind NS1 and was docked onto the NS1 dimer. In human microvascular endothelial cells (HMEC-1), NS1 reduced the relative trans-endothelial electrical resistance to 0.56 (P Conclusions/Significance ASII is a candidate for further study as a modulator of NS1-induced endothelial hyperpermeability. Its mechanism remains unresolved and requires further study. Direct binding to NS1, activity in infected cells and effects in other endothelial models were not tested here, and the computational predictions require experimental confirmation. Author summary Dengue is a viral disease spread by mosquitoes that infects hundreds of millions of people each year. In severe cases, fluid leaks out of the smallest blood vessels, which can cause shock and death. No approved medicine treats the infection itself. A viral protein called NS1, which infected cells release into the blood, helps cause this leakage by damaging the lining of the blood vessels. We therefore looked for a compound that could block the harmful effects of NS1. We trained a computer program to predict which chemicals are likely to stick to a given protein, and we used it to rank 177 natural compounds. We chose one of the best-ranked compounds, astragaloside II, which comes from a plant used in traditional Chinese medicine. In cultures of blood vessel cells, NS1 weakened the cell layer, and astragaloside II prevented this damage without harming the cells. The compound also reduced the binding of NS1 to a sugar-like molecule on cell surfaces, but only weakly at the doses that protected the cells. We did not test it in infected cells or animals, so we regard it as a lead for further work, not as a treatment.

Question and background
Dengue is a mosquito-borne viral disease in which severe cases are marked by leakage of plasma from blood vessels. The secreted nonstructural protein 1 (NS1) of dengue virus damages the endothelial barrier and is a drug target, but no approved antiviral exists.
Design and population
We aimed to identify compounds that target NS1 and to test whether the selected compound, astragaloside II (ASII), counteracts NS1-induced endothelial hyperpermeability. We trained a computer program to predict which chemicals are likely to stick to a given protein, and we used it to rank 177 natural compounds.
Core methods
We built a deep learning model that predicts compound–target binding from chemical fingerprints and protein sequences, and used it to prescreen 177 natural compounds against NS1 of the four dengue virus serotypes. We chose one of the best-ranked compounds, astragaloside II, which comes from a plant used in traditional Chinese medicine.
Main results

Interpretation and novelty

一种工程改造的寨卡病毒重组腺病毒疫苗在小鼠中诱导保护性免疫应答

An engineered Zika virus recombinant adenovirus vaccine induces protective immune response in mice
作者： Hongxia Ni, Jie Chen, Yu-Sha Liu, Yi-Tian Ding, Yiyou Lou, Shuang-Wei Wang, Zi-Cheng Chen, Hao-Xuan Chen, Jin-Rong Tian, Yi-Nan Li
摘要中文翻译摘要 寨卡病毒(ZIKV)、日本脑炎病毒(JEV)和登革病毒(DENV)在许多国家/地区共存。我们之前的研究报告指出,寨卡病毒感染诱导的抗体(Abs)可能会加剧JEV感染小鼠的死亡率,给传统寨卡病毒疫苗的研发带来挑战。在此,我们开发了两种重组腺病毒(Ad5-ZIKV-E WT和Ad5-ZIKV-E mut),分别编码寨卡病毒野生型E蛋白和一个在E蛋白的bc环和融合环区域分别替换五个氨基酸为丙氨酸的E蛋白突变体。Ad5-ZIKV-E mut免疫小鼠诱导的JEV交叉反应性或DENV交叉反应性抗体水平低于Ad5-ZIKV-E WT免疫小鼠。Ad5-ZIKV-E mut诱导的寨卡病毒中和抗体水平与Ad5-ZIKV-E WT诱导的水平相当。Ad5-ZIKV-E mut诱导的抗体增强JEV或DENV感染的能力在体外较Ad5-ZIKV-E WT有所减弱。Ad5-ZIKV-E mut诱导的抗体在体内的保护作用与Ad5-ZIKV-E WT诱导的相似。接受Ad5-ZIKV-E WT免疫小鼠血清的小鼠在DENV挑战后表现出抗体依赖性增强(ADE),而被动转移Ad5-ZIKV-E mut免疫小鼠血清则未在受体小鼠中导致明显的DENV感染ADE。值得注意的是,母源性获得的抗体在Ad5-ZIKV-E mut免疫小鼠所生的2日龄新生小鼠接受JEV挑战后,并未增强其死亡率。这些结果表明,优化寨卡病毒E蛋白的氨基酸序列可以显著降低对JEV和DENV的交叉反应性抗体水平,并消除JEV和DENV感染的ADE风险,为开发一种有效且安全的寨卡病毒疫苗提供了有前景的策略。重要性 寨卡病毒(ZIKV)、日本脑炎病毒(JEV)和登革病毒(DENV)在一些国家或地区共存。初步动物实验和临床队列研究研究的结果支持了寨卡病毒感染诱导的抗体可能在体外和体内增强JEV或DENV感染的观点。因此,通过传统方法构建的编码野生型E蛋白的寨卡病毒疫苗存在针对JEV和DENV感染的抗体依赖性增强风险。在本研究中,我们通过将ZIKV野生型E蛋白的bc环和FL序列中的关键氨基酸替换为丙氨酸,获得了一种E蛋白突变体。基于这种E蛋白突变体的重组腺病毒疫苗不仅在预防寨卡病毒感染方面有效,而且更加安全。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
寨卡病毒(ZIKV)、日本脑炎病毒(JEV)和登革病毒(DENV)在许多国家/地区共存。本研究旨在解决传统寨卡病毒疫苗中由交叉反应性抗体带来的挑战,这些抗体可能加剧JEV或DENV感染。
设计与对象
本研究涉及动物实验和临床队列研究研究,以评估寨卡病毒感染诱导的抗体对JEV和DENV感染的影响。结果显示,寨卡病毒感染诱导的抗体可能在体外和体内增强这些感染。
核心方法
研究人员开发了两种重组腺病毒,分别编码寨卡病毒野生型E蛋白和一个在E蛋白的bc环和融合环区域分别替换五个氨基酸为丙氨酸的E蛋白突变体。随后,他们评估了小鼠中的免疫应答,并在体外和体内评估了抗体依赖性增强(ADE)的潜在风险。
主要结果
Ad5-ZIKV-E mut免疫小鼠诱导的JEV交叉反应性或DENV交叉反应性抗体水平低于Ad5-ZIKV-E WT免疫小鼠。Ad5-ZIKV-E mut诱导的抗体在体外增强JEV或DENV感染的能力也较Ad5-ZIKV-E WT有所减弱。
解释与创新
这些结果表明,优化寨卡病毒E蛋白的氨基酸序列可以显著降低对JEV和DENV的交叉反应性抗体水平,并消除JEV和DENV感染的ADE风险,为开发一种有效且安全的寨卡病毒疫苗提供了有前景的策略。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为疫苗的设计提供了见解,有助于降低针对JEV和DENV的抗体依赖性增强风险,这对于开发更安全、具有更广泛交叉保护能力的寨卡病毒疫苗至关重要。
局限与证据强度
本研究基于动物实验,未包括人体试验,这限制了其对公共卫生的直接适用性。然而,证据是强有力的,并基于体外和体内的实验结果。

An engineered Zika virus recombinant adenovirus vaccine induces protective immune response in mice

Authors: Hongxia Ni, Jie Chen, Yu-Sha Liu, Yi-Tian Ding, Yiyou Lou, Shuang-Wei Wang, Zi-Cheng Chen, Hao-Xuan Chen, Jin-Rong Tian, Yi-Nan Li
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Zika virus (ZIKV), Japanese encephalitis virus (JEV), and dengue virus (DENV) co-circulate in many countries/territories. Our previous publication reported that the antibodies (Abs) induced by ZIKV infection could exacerbate the mortality of JEV-infected mice, bringing challenges to the development of traditional Zika vaccines. Here, we developed two recombinant adenoviruses (Ad5-ZIKV-E WT and Ad5-ZIKV-E mut ) encoding the ZIKV wild-type E protein and an E protein mutant carrying five amino acid substitutions (replaced by alanine) in the bc loop and fusion loop regions of the E protein, respectively. The levels of JEV-cross-reactive or DENV-cross-reactive Ab responses induced by Ad5-ZIKV-E mut immunization in mice are lower than those induced by Ad5-ZIKV-E WT . The level of ZIKV-neutralizing Abs induced by Ad5-ZIKV-E mut is comparable to that elicited by Ad5-ZIKV-E WT . The ability of Abs induced by Ad5-ZIKV-E mut to enhance JEV or DENV infections in vitro is attenuated as compared with that induced by Ad5-ZIKV-E WT . The in vivo protective role of Abs induced by Ad5-ZIKV-E mut is similar to that induced by Ad5-ZIKV-E WT . Mice receiving Ad5-ZIKV-E WT -immune mouse sera exhibited antibody-dependent enhancement (ADE) post-DENV challenge, whereas passively transferred Ad5-ZIKV-E mut -immune mouse sera did not lead to obvious ADE of DENV infection in recipient mice. Notably, maternally acquired Abs did not enhance the mortality of 2-day-old neonates born to Ad5-ZIKV-E mut -immunized mice post-JEV challenge. These results suggest that optimizing the E protein amino acid sequence of ZIKV could significantly reduce the level of cross-reactive Abs to JEV and DENV, and abrogate the ADE of JEV and DENV infections, providing a promising strategy to develop an effective and safe ZIKV vaccine. IMPORTANCE Zika virus (ZIKV), Japanese encephalitis virus (JEV), and dengue virus (DENV) co-circulate in some countries or territories. The results of preliminary animal experiments and clinical cohort studies support that antibodies induced by ZIKV infection can enhance JEV or DENV infection in vitro and in vivo . Therefore, the ZIKV vaccine encoding the wild-type E protein constructed by conventional methods has the antibody-dependent enhancement risk against JEV and DENV infections. In this study, we obtained an E protein mutant by mutating key amino acids in the bc loop and FL sequences of the ZIKV wild-type E protein to alanine. The recombinant adenovirus vaccine based on this E protein mutant is not only effective but also safer in preventing ZIKV infection in the future.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Zika virus (ZIKV), Japanese encephalitis virus (JEV), and dengue virus (DENV) co-circulate in many countries/territories. The study aimed to address the challenges posed by cross-reactive antibodies in traditional ZIKV vaccines that may exacerbate JEV or DENV infections.
Design and population
The study involved animal experiments and clinical cohort studies to evaluate the effects of ZIKV-induced antibodies on JEV and DENV infections. The results showed that antibodies from ZIKV infection could enhance these infections in vitro and in vivo.
Core methods
The researchers developed two recombinant adenoviruses encoding the ZIKV wild-type E protein and an E protein mutant with five amino acid substitutions in the bc loop and fusion loop regions. They then assessed the immune responses in mice and evaluated the potential for antibody-dependent enhancement (ADE) in vitro and in vivo.
Main results
The levels of JEV-cross-reactive or DENV-cross-reactive antibody responses induced by Ad5-ZIKV-E mut immunization in mice are lower than those induced by Ad5-ZIKV-E WT. The ability of antibodies induced by Ad5-ZIKV-E mut to enhance JEV or DENV infections in vitro is also attenuated compared with those induced by Ad5-ZIKV-E WT.
Interpretation and novelty
The results suggest that optimizing the E protein amino acid sequence of ZIKV could significantly reduce the level of cross-reactive antibodies to JEV and DENV, and abrogate the ADE of JEV and DENV infections, providing a promising strategy to develop an effective and safe ZIKV vaccine.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings offer insights into vaccine design that could mitigate the risk of antibody-dependent enhancement against JEV and DENV, which is crucial for developing safer ZIKV vaccines with broader cross-protection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on animal experiments and does not include human trials, which limits its direct applicability to public health. However, the evidence is strong and grounded in in vitro and in vivo experimental results.

通过 CRISPR 工具和药理抑制来靶向 CLK1 可调节先天的 免疫应答 和 登革病毒 复制。

Targeting CLK1 by CRISPR tools and pharmacological inhibition modulates the innate immune response and dengue virus replication.
作者： Berta Pozzi, Agustina Magalí Lucero, Valeria Buggiano, Miguel Angel Pelaez, Cybele Carina García, Anabella Srebrow
摘要中文翻译选择性剪接是已知在病毒感染时发生改变的关键调节机制。这些改变可能来自病毒对剪接机制的直接干扰,也可能来自细胞反应,例如先天免疫的激活。在这里,我们研究了培养细胞中 登革病毒 (DENV) 感染和干扰素 (IFN) 治疗 之间共享的剪接变化,后者作为病毒依赖性先天免疫激活的模型。在常见事件中,我们发现 CLK1 基因非编码 mRNA 亚型的产量增加,从而产生一种磷酸化剪接因子(包括 SR 蛋白和剪接体成分)的激酶。与这一发现一致,IFN 治疗 导致 CLK1 蛋白水平降低。使用具有 CRISPR/dCas9 介导的 CLK1 表达调节的稳定细胞系,我们发现沉默 CLK1 增强了 免疫应答 基因的诱导,而其过度表达则减弱了这种诱导。用药理学抑制剂 TG003 抑制 CLK1 激酶活性,进一步增强 IFN 诱导的基因表达并减少 DENV 复制。总而言之,这些结果确定 CLK1 是 IFN 刺激的基因表达的前病毒负调节因子,并表明其抑制可以增强 抗病毒药物 防御并成为治疗策略的目标。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨病毒感染和干扰素 治疗 如何影响剪接变化,因为选择性剪接是病毒感染过程中被改变的关键调控机制。
设计与对象
本研究使用培养的细胞来研究 登革病毒(登革病毒)感染和干扰素(IFN) 治疗 对剪接变化的影响,其中 IFN 治疗 作为病毒独立的先天免疫激活模型。
核心方法
研究人员使用 CRISPR/dCas9 工具调节 CLK1 表达,并使用药物抑制剂评估 CLK1 激酶活性对 免疫应答 基因和登革病毒复制的影响。
主要结果
研究发现,在登革病毒感染和 IFN 治疗 激活过程中,CLK1 基因表达均增加,但 IFN 治疗 导致 CLK1 蛋白水平下降。沉默 CLK1 增强了 免疫应答 基因的诱导,而其过表达则抑制了该诱导。使用 TG003 抑制 CLK1 激酶活性进一步增强了干扰素诱导的基因表达,并减少了登革病毒的复制。
解释与创新
这些发现表明 CLK1 在干扰素刺激基因表达中起负调控作用,其抑制可能有助于增强 抗病毒药物 防御能力。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为针对 CLK1 的潜在治疗策略提供了见解,以增强 抗病毒药物 对登革病毒的防御能力。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于培养细胞,可能无法完全代表体内情况,且证据基于实验性调节而非直接观察病毒在人体中的行为。证据强度为中等。

Targeting CLK1 by CRISPR tools and pharmacological inhibition modulates the innate immune response and dengue virus replication.

Authors: Berta Pozzi, Agustina Magalí Lucero, Valeria Buggiano, Miguel Angel Pelaez, Cybele Carina García, Anabella Srebrow
Original abstract (English)
Alternative splicing is a key regulatory mechanism known to be altered upon viral infection. These alterations can arise both from direct viral interference with the splicing machinery and from cellular responses such as activation of innate immunity. Here, we investigated splicing changes shared between dengue virus (DENV) infection and interferon (IFN) treatment in cultured cells, the latter serving as a model for virus-independent innate immune activation. Among the common events, we identified an increased production of non-coding mRNA isoforms from the CLK1 gene, which gives rise to a kinase that phosphorylates splicing factors including SR proteins and spliceosomal components. Consistent with this finding, IFN treatment led to a reduction in CLK1 protein levels. Using stable cell lines with CRISPR/dCas9-mediated modulation of CLK1 expression, we found that silencing CLK1 enhanced the induction of immune response genes, while its overexpression attenuated it. Inhibition of CLK1 kinase activity with the pharmacological inhibitor TG003 further potentiated IFN-induced gene expression and reduced DENV replication. Altogether, these results identify CLK1 as a proviral negative regulator of IFN-stimulated gene expression and suggest that its inhibition could enhance antiviral defenses and become a target for therapeutic strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore how viral infection and interferon treatment affect splicing changes, as alternative splicing is a key regulatory mechanism altered during viral infection.
Design and population
The study used cultured cells to investigate splicing changes in dengue virus (DENV) infection and interferon (IFN) treatment, with IFN treatment serving as a model for virus-independent innate immune activation.
Core methods
The researchers employed CRISPR/dCas9 tools to modulate CLK1 expression and pharmacological inhibitors to assess the impact of CLK1 kinase activity on immune response genes and DENV replication.
Main results
The study found that CLK1 gene expression increased during DENV infection and IFN treatment, with IFN treatment leading to a reduction in CLK1 protein levels. Silencing CLK1 enhanced immune response gene induction, while its overexpression attenuated it. Inhibition of CLK1 kinase activity with TG003 further potentiated IFN-induced gene expression and reduced DENV replication.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that CLK1 acts as a negative regulator of IFN-stimulated gene expression and that its inhibition could enhance antiviral defenses.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide insights into potential therapeutic strategies targeting CLK1 to improve antiviral defenses against dengue virus.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to cultured cells, which may not fully represent in vivo conditions, and the evidence is based on experimental modulation rather than direct observation of viral behavior in humans. The evidence strength is moderate.

血清非靶向代谢组学揭示寨卡病毒、登革病毒和流感A病毒感染期间感染相关的代谢改变。

Serum untargeted metabolomics reveals infection-associated metabolic alterations in chikungunya, dengue, and influenza A virus infections.
作者： Fen Ouyang, Xinyi Chen, Menghe Wang, Xiaoyue Huang, Huanxia Xu, Yingyun Cheng, Yaqiu Long, Min He
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
寨卡病毒(CHIKV)、登革病毒(DENV)和流感A病毒(IAV)感染在早期临床阶段可能表现出重叠的发热症状。常规实验室检测提供了有用的临床信息,但无法完全捕捉不同病毒感染相关的循环代谢改变。
设计与对象
在本项比较横断面研究中,采用基于液相色谱-质谱(LC-MS)的非靶向代谢组学方法分析了CHIKV、DENV或IAV感染患者及健康对照的血清样本。
核心方法
数据在正离子和负离子电喷雾电离模式下获取,并对数据集进行处理以表征感染相关的代谢改变。进行了质量控制评估、主成分分析、正交偏最小二乘判别分析、使用Benjamini-Hochberg校正的差异代谢物筛选、京都基因与基因组百科全书(KEGG)通路分类以及受试者工作特征(ROC)分析。
主要结果
Thousands of metabolic features were detected across positive and negative ion mode datasets, and annotated features were retained for downstream comparative analyses. Principal component analysis revealed group-dependent metabolic clustering with partial overlap between healthy controls and viral infection groups. Compared with healthy controls, 319, 300, and 295 differential metabolic features were identified in the CHIKV, DENV, and IAV groups, respectively, in positive ion mode, whereas 52, 40, and 33 differential features were identified in negative ion mode.
解释与创新
CHIKV、DENV和IAV感染与健康对照相比,与广泛的血清代谢改变相关。在当前数据集中,CHIKV与对照组的比较产生了最多的代谢改变特征,但这一观察结果应谨慎解读,因为样本量比例不均且缺乏感染组之间的直接比较。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能有助于开发更准确的诊断工具,并提供关于病毒感染代谢机制的见解,从而有助于制定针对性的治疗策略。
局限与证据强度
鉴于样本量相对较小、单中心和单模式设计以及缺乏独立验证数据集,本研究中鉴定的候选代谢物应被视为探索性结果,并需在更大的独立数据集和靶向代谢组学检测中进行验证。

Serum untargeted metabolomics reveals infection-associated metabolic alterations in chikungunya, dengue, and influenza A virus infections.

Authors: Fen Ouyang, Xinyi Chen, Menghe Wang, Xiaoyue Huang, Huanxia Xu, Yingyun Cheng, Yaqiu Long, Min He
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Chikungunya virus (CHIKV), dengue virus (DENV), and influenza A virus (IAV) infections may present with overlapping febrile symptoms during the early clinical phase. Routine laboratory tests provide useful clinical information but do not fully capture the circulating metabolic alterations associated with different viral infections. This study aimed to characterize serum metabolic profiles in CHIKV, DENV, and IAV infections using liquid chromatography-mass spectrometry (LC-MS)-based untargeted metabolomics. METHODS: In this comparative cross-sectional study, serum samples from patients with CHIKV, DENV, or IAV infection and healthy controls were analyzed using liquid chromatography-mass spectrometry (LC-MS)-based untargeted metabolomics. Data were acquired in both positive and negative electrospray ionization modes, and the datasets were processed to characterize infection-associated metabolic alterations. Quality-control assessment, principal component analysis, orthogonal partial least-squares discriminant analysis, differential metabolite screening with Benjamini-Hochberg false discovery rate correction, Kyoto Encyclopedia of Genes and Genomes (KEGG) pathway classification, and receiver operating characteristic analysis were performed. RESULTS: Thousands of metabolic features were detected across positive and negative ion mode datasets, and annotated features were retained for downstream comparative analyses. Principal component analysis revealed group-dependent metabolic clustering with partial overlap between healthy controls and viral infection groups. Compared with healthy controls, 319, 300, and 295 differential metabolic features were identified in the CHIKV, DENV, and IAV groups, respectively, in positive ion mode, whereas 52, 40, and 33 differential features were identified in negative ion mode. Downregulated features predominated in the positive-ion comparisons, while the direction of metabolic alterations varied across groups in negative ion mode. KEGG pathway classification showed that differential metabolites were mapped to several major categories, including lipid metabolism, amino acid metabolism, metabolism of cofactors and vitamins, xenobiotics biodegradation and metabolism, and chemical structure transformation maps. Exploratory receiver operating characteristic (ROC) analysis within the discovery cohort identified multiple metabolites with high discriminatory performance for distinguishing viral infection groups from healthy controls, although these findings were not independently validated. Additional mode-specific analyses further characterized shared differential metabolic features, their directional changes, and the distribution of candidate metabolites with high AUC values. CONCLUSIONS: CHIKV, DENV, and IAV infections were associated with broad serum metabolic alterations compared with healthy controls. The CHIKV-versus-control comparison yielded the largest number of altered metabolic features in the current dataset, although this observation should be interpreted cautiously because of unequal sampling proportions and the absence of direct comparisons between infection groups. Given the relatively small sample size, single-center and single-outbreak design, and lack of an independent validation cohort, the candidate metabolites identified in this study should be regarded as exploratory and require validation in larger independent cohorts and targeted metabolomic assays.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background

Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the development of more accurate diagnostic tools and provide insights into the metabolic mechanisms underlying viral infections, which could aid in targeted therapeutic strategies.

印度尼西亚占米市2010年至2024年间登革热的空间和时空聚集特征

Spatial and Spatiotemporal Clustering of Dengue in Jambi Municipality, Indonesia, 2010–2024
作者： Muhammad Syukri, Marko Salim, Indra Dwinata, AZIZAN BILLARDI
摘要中文翻译背景与目标:登革热是印度尼西亚的公共卫生问题,特别是在城市化进程较快的城市中,登革热的传播模式在空间上具有异质性且季节性动态变化。本研究旨在识别印度尼西亚占米市登革热病例的空间和时空聚集区域。采用回顾性生态学研究方法,使用了覆盖62个城区村庄的登革热传播模式数据。方法:从占米市卫生办公室获取了2010年1月1日至2024年12月31日期间通过常规传播模式报告的每月临床诊断登革热病例数。人口数据来源于中央统计局,病例分析在城区村庄层面进行以支持本地传播模式。采用SaTScan TM软件,使用离散泊松模型进行回顾性空间和时空聚集分析。通过蒙特卡洛假设检验评估统计显著性,并使用Quantum GIS对聚集区域进行可视化。结果:共报告了7,099例临床诊断的登革热病例。纯空间分析和时空分析分别识别出24个和39个显著聚集区域。反复出现的高风险聚集区域主要集中在占米市的中部和南部,尽管其位置每年有所不同。在39个时空聚集区域中,有37个出现在至少一个月的雨季(10月至4月),另外两个次要聚集区域则局限于旱季(2011年8月和2023年6月)。解释与结论:在15年间,登革热表现出反复的小区域聚集,并与雨季月份有显著重叠,这表明需要进行精细尺度的传播模式监测和空间针对性的传播模式控制。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热是印度尼西亚的公共卫生问题,特别是在城市化进程较快的城市中,登革热的传播模式在空间上具有异质性且季节性动态变化。本研究旨在识别印度尼西亚占米市登革热病例的空间和时空聚集区域。
设计与对象
采用回顾性生态学研究方法,使用了覆盖62个城区村庄的登革热传播模式数据。人口数据来源于中央统计局,病例分析在城区村庄层面进行以支持本地传播模式。采用SaTScan TM软件,使用离散泊松模型进行回顾性空间和时空聚集分析。
核心方法
从占米市卫生办公室获取了2010年1月1日至2024年12月31日期间通过常规传播模式报告的每月临床诊断登革热病例数。通过蒙特卡洛假设检验评估统计显著性,并使用Quantum GIS对聚集区域进行可视化。
主要结果
共报告了7,099例临床诊断的登革热病例。纯空间分析和时空分析分别识别出24个和39个显著聚集区域。反复出现的高风险聚集区域主要集中在占米市的中部和南部,尽管其位置每年有所不同。在39个时空聚集区域中,有37个出现在至少一个月的雨季(10月至4月),另外两个次要聚集区域则局限于旱季(2011年8月和2023年6月)。
解释与创新
在15年间,登革热表现出反复的小区域聚集,并与雨季月份有显著重叠,这表明需要进行精细尺度的传播模式监测和空间针对性的传播模式控制。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,需要进行精细尺度的传播模式监测和空间针对性的传播模式控制,以应对印度尼西亚占米市登革热的传播模式。
局限与证据强度
本研究受限于其回顾性设计和对聚合传播模式数据的依赖,这可能无法捕捉个体层面的变化。由于基于生态学数据和统计分析,证据强度中等。

Spatial and Spatiotemporal Clustering of Dengue in Jambi Municipality, Indonesia, 2010–2024

Authors: Muhammad Syukri, Marko Salim, Indra Dwinata, AZIZAN BILLARDI
Original abstract (English)
Background & objectives: Dengue is a public health problem in Indonesia, particularly in urbanising cities where transmission patterns are spatially heterogeneous and seasonally dynamic. This study aimed to identify the spatial and spatiotemporal clusters of dengue cases in Jambi Municipality, Indonesia. A retrospective ecological study was conducted using dengue surveillance data aggregated across 62 urban villages. Methods: Monthly counts of clinically diagnosed dengue cases reported through routine surveillance between 1 January 2010 and 31 December 2024 were obtained from the Jambi Municipality Health Office. Population data were obtained from the Central Bureau of Statistics and cases were analysed at the urban-village level to support local surveillance. Retrospective spatial and space-time cluster analyses were carried out using SaTScan TM with a discrete Poisson model. Monte Carlo hypothesis testing was used to assess statistical significance, and the clusters were visualised with Quantum GIS. Results: A total of 7,099 clinically diagnosed dengue cases were reported. Purely spatial and space-time analyses identified 24 and 39 significant clusters, respectively. Recurrent high-risk clusters were concentrated mainly in the central and southern parts of the municipality, although their locations varied by year. Among the 39 space-time clusters, 37 were in a minimum of one rainy-season month (October-April), and the other two secondary clusters were limited to the dry season (August 2011 and June 2023. Interpretation & conclusion: Dengue showed recurrent small-area clustering and significant overlap with rainy-season months over 15 years, suggesting the need for fine-scale surveillance and spatially targeted vector control.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue is a public health problem in Indonesia, particularly in urbanising cities where transmission patterns are spatially heterogeneous and seasonally dynamic. This study aimed to identify the spatial and spatiotemporal clusters of dengue cases in Jambi Municipality, Indonesia.
Design and population
A retrospective ecological study was conducted using dengue surveillance data aggregated across 62 urban villages. Recurrent high-risk clusters were concentrated mainly in the central and southern parts of the municipality, although their locations varied by year. Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest the need for fine-scale surveillance and spatially targeted vector control to address dengue outbreaks in Jambi Municipality, Indonesia.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and reliance on aggregated surveillance data, which may not capture individual-level variations. The evidence is moderate as it is based on ecological data and statistical analysis.

TRIM5α、TRIM56和TRIM69通过选择性NS2B3介导的拮抗作用差异性调控登革病毒 2感染

TRIM5α, TRIM56 and TRIM69 differentially regulate dengue virus 2 infection through selective NS2B3-mediated antagonism
作者： Ricky Raj Paswan, Luan Letieri Belem Martins, Natacha Mérindol, Soléna Rossi, Aïssatou Aïcha Sow, Nguyễn Hà Nhung, Jyotika Singh, Laurent Chatel‐Chaix, Isabel Desgagné‐Penix, Lionel Berthoux
摘要中文翻译登革病毒(登革病毒,DENV)是一种主要的蚊媒人类病原体。理解细胞对感染的抵抗机制对于开发新的治疗工具至关重要。三结构域(TRIM)蛋白通过直接限制病毒复制和调控抗病毒药物信号通路参与抗病毒药物先天免疫,但目前关于TRIM蛋白对DENV靶向作用的知识仍处于初步阶段。此前发现TRIM56能够抑制DENV,但关于TRIM69是否为DENV限制因子的报道存在矛盾。令人惊讶的是,广谱病毒限制因子TRIM5α被提出可限制蜱媒但非蚊媒黄病毒。在此,我们研究了TRIM5α、TRIM56和TRIM69对登革病毒2型(DENV2)感染的影响。过表达实验表明TRIM5α和TRIM56限制DENV2感染,而TRIM69则增强感染。一致地,CRISPR/Cas9介导的失能实验显示敲除TRIM5α或TRIM56会增加感染,而敲除TRIM69则会减少感染。限制作用与TRIM5α和TRIM56表达细胞中病毒RNA和病毒蛋白水平的降低相关。病毒无感染实验表明TRIM56可下调DENV2蛋白酶复合物NS2B3的水平。相反,DENV2 NS2B3可独立于蛋白酶活性降低TRIM56和TRIM69的丰度。RING结构域突变的TRIM56保留了部分抗病毒药物活性,并且仍能降低NS2B3水平,TRIM56-NS2B3的相互下调效应并未被蛋白酶体或溶酶体抑制所抵消。综上所述,这些发现确立了TRIM5α作为DENV2的限制因子,揭示了TRIM69在DENV2感染中的正向作用,表明TRIM5α和TRIM56通过不同的机制抑制DENV2,并提示TRIM56-NS2B3的相互下调可能不依赖于蛋白酶体。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革病毒(登革病毒,DENV)是一种主要的蚊媒人类病原体。理解细胞对感染的抵抗机制对于开发新的治疗工具至关重要。
设计与对象
在此,我们研究了TRIM5α、TRIM56和TRIM69对登革病毒2型(DENV2)感染的影响。研究聚焦于DENV2,并探讨TRIM蛋白在其感染中的作用。
核心方法
过表达实验表明TRIM5α和TRIM56限制DENV2感染,而TRIM69则增强感染。一致地,CRISPR/Cas9介导的失能实验显示敲除TRIM5α或TRIM56会增加感染,而敲除TRIM69则会减少感染。
主要结果
限制作用与TRIM5α和TRIM56表达细胞中病毒RNA和病毒蛋白水平的降低相关。
解释与创新
综上所述,这些发现确立了TRIM5α作为DENV2的限制因子,揭示了TRIM69在DENV2感染中的正向作用,表明TRIM5α和TRIM56通过不同的机制抑制DENV2,并提示TRIM56-NS2B3的相互下调可能不依赖于蛋白酶体。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为理解DENV2限制的分子机制提供了见解,并可能为针对TRIM蛋白的策略开发提供信息,以预防和控制登革病毒感染。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告任何可能影响研究结果可信度的局限性或设计相关问题。

TRIM5α, TRIM56 and TRIM69 differentially regulate dengue virus 2 infection through selective NS2B3-mediated antagonism

Authors: Ricky Raj Paswan, Luan Letieri Belem Martins, Natacha Mérindol, Soléna Rossi, Aïssatou Aïcha Sow, Nguyễn Hà Nhung, Jyotika Singh, Laurent Chatel‐Chaix, Isabel Desgagné‐Penix, Lionel Berthoux
Original abstract (English)
Dengue virus (DENV) is a major mosquito-borne human pathogen. Understanding the mechanisms of cellular resistance to the infection is vital to develop novel therapeutic tools. Tripartite motif (TRIM) proteins contribute to antiviral innate immunity through direct restriction of viral replication and regulation of antiviral signaling, but current knowledge on DENV targeting by TRIM proteins remains preliminary. Previously, TRIM56 was found to inhibit DENV, whereas reports on whether TRIM69 is a DENV restriction factor are conflicting. Surprisingly, the broad-spectrum viral restriction factor TRIM5α was proposed to restrict tick-borne but not mosquito-borne flaviviruses. Here, we investigated the effects of TRIM5α, TRIM56, and TRIM69 on DENV serotype 2 (DENV2) infection. Overexpression experiments showed that TRIM5α and TRIM56 restrict DENV2, whereas TRIM69 enhances it. Consistently, CRISPR/Cas9-mediated loss-of-function showed that knocking out TRIM5α or TRIM56 increases infection, while TRIM69 knockout decreases it. Restriction correlated with reduced viral RNA and viral protein levels in TRIM5α- and TRIM56-expressing cells. Virus-free experiments showed that TRIM56 downregulates the DENV2 protease complex NS2B3 levels. Reciprocally, DENV2 NS2B3 reduced TRIM56 and TRIM69 abundance independently of protease activity. RING domain-mutated TRIM56 retained partial antiviral activity and the ability to reduce NS2B3 levels, and TRIM56-NS2B3 reciprocal downmodulation effects were not counteracted by proteasomal or lysosomal inhibition. Together, these findings establish TRIM5α as a DENV2 restriction factor, reveal TRIM69 as a positive factor in DENV2 infection, suggest that TRIM5? and TRIM56 inhibit DENV2 through distinct mechanisms, and hint at proteasome-independent TRIM56-NS2B3 reciprocal downmodulation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue virus (DENV) is a major mosquito-borne human pathogen. Understanding the mechanisms of cellular resistance to the infection is vital to develop novel therapeutic tools.
Design and population
Here, we investigated the effects of TRIM5α, TRIM56, and TRIM69 on DENV serotype 2 (DENV2) infection. The study focused on DENV2 and examined the role of TRIM proteins in its infection.
Core methods
Overexpression experiments showed that TRIM5α and TRIM56 restrict DENV2, whereas TRIM69 enhances it. Consistently, CRISPR/Cas9-mediated loss-of-function showed that knocking out TRIM5α or TRIM56 increases infection, while TRIM69 knockout decreases it.
Main results
Restriction correlated with reduced viral RNA and viral protein levels in TRIM5α- and TRIM56-expressing cells.
Interpretation and novelty
Together, these findings establish TRIM5α as a DENV2 restriction factor, reveal TRIM69 as a positive factor in DENV2 infection, suggest that TRIM5α and TRIM56 inhibit DENV2 through distinct mechanisms, and hint at proteasome-independent TRIM56-NS2B3 reciprocal downmodulation.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings provide insights into the molecular mechanisms of DENV2 restriction and may inform the development of antiviral strategies targeting TRIM proteins for the prevention and treatment of dengue infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or design-related issues that would affect the confidence in the findings.

登革病毒包膜蛋白及其跨物种传播机制

Mutation of Dengue Virus Envelope Protein and Mechanism of Cross-Host Transmission
作者： Xin-Yin Zhang
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在了解登革病毒在包膜蛋白中的突变如何促进其跨物种传播能力。登革病毒在全球范围内传播,对公共卫生构成重大风险。
设计与对象
本研究聚焦于登革病毒在蚊子和人类之间传播的分子机制,分析其包膜蛋白及突变模式在两种宿主中的情况。
核心方法
研究涉及对E蛋白结构的梳理以及通过分子分析确定突变热点位点,解释单个氨基酸变化如何影响病毒的耐热性、受体结合速度和细胞内复制能力。
主要结果
Most adaptive non-synonymous mutations occur on the gene that encodes the E protein, and these amino acid changes reshape the protein’s 3D structure, enabling the virus to jump across different host species.
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为一条分子路径,说明登革病毒如何在蚊子和人类之间传播,为理解其跨物种传播机制提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以用于疫情预警系统,指导广谱抗病毒药物的设计,并支持新型抗病毒药物的研发。
局限与证据强度
研究存在局限性,包括对野生病毒株的长期追踪不足,以及实验室细胞模型无法完全复制真实感染环境。证据强度中等。

Mutation of Dengue Virus Envelope Protein and Mechanism of Cross-Host Transmission

Authors: Xin-Yin Zhang
Original abstract (English)
Dengue virus spreads worldwide. Mosquitoes of the Aedes genus pass it to humans. The virus poses big risks to global public health. Its envelope protein, E protein, controls three key viral behaviors. It lets viruses recognize host cells, fuse cell membranes and live through changing environments. Most adaptive non-synonymous mutations appear on the gene that makes E protein. These amino acid changes reshape the protein’s 3D structure. This change lets dengue jump across different host species. This paper sorts out E protein structures and sites where mutations often happen. It explains how single amino acid changes affect virus heat resistance, receptor binding speed and intracellular replication. Mutated E proteins help viruses escape mosquito immune defenses. They also help viruses survive stress inside mammalian cells. This paper builds a full molecular path to explain how dengue shifts between mosquitoes and people. Current research has clear limits. Few studies measure how multiple mutations work together. Lab cell models cannot copy real infection environments. Long-term tracking of wild virus strains is rare. This work improves theories about dengue’s evolution in two hosts. It picks out key adaptive mutation sites. It offers theory support for epidemic early warning, broad-spectrum vaccine design and new antiviral drug research.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to understand how mutations in the Dengue virus envelope protein contribute to its ability to transmit across different hosts. Dengue virus spreads worldwide, posing significant public health risks.
Design and population
The study focused on the molecular mechanisms of Dengue virus transmission between mosquitoes and humans, analyzing its envelope protein and mutation patterns in both hosts.
Core methods
The research involved sorting out E protein structures and identifying mutation sites through molecular analysis, explaining how single amino acid changes affect heat resistance, receptor binding speed and intracellular replication.
Main results
Most adaptive non-synonymous mutations occur on the gene that encodes the E protein, and these amino acid changes reshape the protein’s 3D structure, enabling the virus to jump across different host species.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as a molecular pathway explaining how Dengue virus shifts between mosquitoes and humans, offering new insights into its cross-host transmission mechanism.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform epidemic early warning systems, guide the design of broad-spectrum vaccines and support the development of new antiviral drugs.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study has limitations, including the lack of long-term tracking of wild virus strains and the inability of lab cell models to fully replicate real infection environments. The evidence is moderate.

全球登革热、气候与极端天气研究中的差异:与疾病负担和气候驱动的媒介适宜性的一致性文献计量分析

Global disparities in dengue, climate, and extreme weather research: a bibliometric analysis of alignment with disease burden and climate-driven vector suitability
作者： Chunyue Ai, Yi Teng, Guangju Mo, Wenyu Wang, Meshack Kipkogei Biwott, Jing Li, Qiyong Liu
摘要中文翻译背景:登革热是一种气候敏感的媒介传播疾病,在热带和亚热带地区对公共卫生构成重大负担。温度、降雨和极端天气会影响蚊虫生态、人类暴露以及传播的环境适宜性。本研究描述了登革热和极端天气的全球研究格局,并检查了国家层面的研究产出是否与登革热负担和气候驱动的媒介适宜性相一致。方法:于2026年5月22日搜索Web of Science核心数据库。使用CiteSpace、VOSviewer和bibliometrix R包分析出版趋势、合作网络、期刊、作者、共同引用参考文献、引用爆发、关键词模式和主题进化。国家层面的出版产出与2023年全球疾病负担数据集、2021年社会人口指数以及1990-2022年平均指数-p值进行了关联。使用Spearman等级相关分析评估出版产出与登革热负担之间的关联。结果:2008年至2026年间共有290篇文献符合纳入标准。总体年度出版产出呈上升趋势,并且在2020年后相对较高。美国贡献了最多的文献,其次是中华人民共和国、英国、澳大利亚和巴西。文献将热带医学和传染病流行病学与环境健康、气候科学、媒介生态、建模和公共卫生联系起来。主要主题包括气候变化、温度、降雨、厄尔尼诺-南方涛动、极端天气、Aedes 媒介、传播建模、风险预测和早期预警。在80个被归类为登革热流行地区的国家中,出版产出与2023年的年龄标准化发病率率(ASIR)呈正相关(Spearman's ρ = 0.32,P = 0.004,n = 80),与2023年的伤残调整生命年(DALY)率呈正相关(ρ = 0.36,P < 0.001,n = 80)。相比之下,其与1990年至2023年ASIR的估计年百分比变化(EAPC)之间的关联并不具有统计学意义(ρ = 0.20,P = 0.070,n = 80)。在当前数据集中,一些登革热流行地区国家尽管疾病负担较高或长期气候驱动的媒介适宜性较高,但没有符合纳入标准的Web of Science索引文献。结论:登革热与极端天气的研究已有所扩展,出版产出与登革热流行地区的当前疾病负担显示出适度的正相关。然而,研究产出与登革热发病率的长期趋势无显著关联,且仍存在显著的地理差异。对疾病负担、研究产出和气候驱动的媒介适宜性进行单独评估,识别了可能需要在发展研究能力、监测支持和公平的国际合作伙伴关系时进一步关注的地理区域。局限性与证据强度:提供的证据未报告设计局限性、偏倚、混杂因素、样本量、控制措施、可推广性或仅摘要的证据。使用Spearman等级相关分析评估出版产出与登革热负担之间的关联,这可能无法捕捉复杂的关系。证据强度为中等。

提供的证据未报告设计局限性、偏倚、混杂因素、样本量、控制措施、可推广性或仅摘要的证据。使用Spearman等级相关分析评估出版产出与登革热负担之间的关联,这可能无法捕捉复杂的关系。证据强度为中等。

Global disparities in dengue, climate, and extreme weather research: a bibliometric analysis of alignment with disease burden and climate-driven vector suitability

Authors: Chunyue Ai, Yi Teng, Guangju Mo, Wenyu Wang, Meshack Kipkogei Biwott, Jing Li, Qiyong Liu
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Dengue is a climate-sensitive vector-borne disease that imposes a substantial public health burden in tropical and subtropical regions. Temperature, rainfall, and extreme weather can affect mosquito ecology, human exposure, and the environmental suitability for transmission. This study characterized the global research landscape of dengue and extreme weather and examined whether country-level research output corresponded to dengue burden and climate-driven vector suitability. METHODS: The Web of Science Core Collection was searched on 22 May 2026. Publication trends, collaboration networks, journals, authors, co-cited references, citation bursts, keyword patterns, and thematic evolution were examined using CiteSpace, VOSviewer, and the bibliometrix R package. Country-level publication output was linked to estimates from the Global Burden of Disease 2023 dataset, the socio-demographic index for 2021, and mean index-p values for 1990-2022. Associations between publication output and dengue burden were assessed using Spearman's rank correlation among countries classified as dengue-endemic. RESULTS: A total of 290 publications from 2008 to 2026 met the eligibility criteria. Annual publication output increased overall and remained comparatively high after 2020. The United States contributed the largest number of publications, followed by China, the United Kingdom, Australia, and Brazil. The literature connected tropical medicine and infectious disease epidemiology with environmental health, climate science, vector ecology, modeling, and public health. Major themes included climate change, temperature, rainfall, the El Niño-Southern Oscillation, extreme weather, Aedes vectors, transmission modeling, risk prediction, and early warning. Among 80 countries classified as dengue-endemic, publication output was positively correlated with the age-standardized incidence rate (ASIR) in 2023 (Spearman's ρ = 0.32, P = 0.004, n = 80) and the disability-adjusted life-year (DALY) rate in 2023 (ρ = 0.36, P < 0.001, n = 80). In contrast, its association with the estimated annual percentage change (EAPC) in ASIR from 1990 to 2023 was not statistically significant (ρ = 0.20, P = 0.070, n = 80). Several dengue-endemic countries with comparatively high disease burden or long-term climate-driven vector suitability had no eligible Web of Science-indexed publications in the present dataset. CONCLUSIONS: Research on dengue and extreme weather has expanded, and publication output showed modest positive associations with contemporary dengue burden among dengue-endemic countries. However, research output was not significantly associated with the long-term trend in dengue incidence, and substantial geographic disparities remained. The separate assessment of burden, research output, and climate-driven vector suitability identified geographic areas that may warrant closer consideration when research capacity, surveillance support, and equitable international partnerships are developed.

Question and background
Dengue is a climate-sensitive vector-borne disease that imposes a substantial public health burden in tropical and subtropical regions. Temperature, rainfall, and extreme weather can affect mosquito ecology, human exposure, and the environmental suitability for transmission.

Country-level publication output was linked to estimates from the Global Burden of Disease 2023 dataset, the socio-demographic index for 2021, and mean index-p values for 1990-2022. Associations between publication output and dengue burden were assessed using Spearman's rank correlation among countries classified as dengue-endemic.
Main results
A total of 290 publications from 2008 to 2026 met the eligibility criteria. Annual publication output increased overall and remained comparatively high after 2020. The United States contributed the largest number of publications, followed by China, the United Kingdom, Australia, and Brazil. Several dengue-endemic countries with comparatively high disease burden or long-term climate-driven vector suitability had no eligible Web of Science-indexed publications in the present dataset.

Research on dengue and extreme weather has expanded, and publication output showed modest positive associations with contemporary dengue burden among dengue-endemic countries. However, research output was not significantly associated with the long-term trend in dengue incidence, and substantial geographic disparities remained.

The literature connected tropical medicine and infectious disease epidemiology with environmental health, climate science, vector ecology, modeling, and public health. The separate assessment of burden, research output, and climate-driven vector suitability identified geographic areas that may warrant closer consideration when research capacity, surveillance support, and equitable international partnerships are developed.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report on design limitations, bias, confounding, sample size, controls, generalisability, or abstract-only evidence. The association between publication output and dengue burden was assessed using Spearman's rank correlation, which may not capture complex relationships. The evidence strength is moderate.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

Chandipura病毒和登革病毒病毒感染期间宿主细胞丝氨酸和精氨酸丰富(SR)蛋白及凋亡因子BCL-2基因表达的差异调控

Differential modulation of host cell Serine and Arginine-Rich (SR) protein and apoptotic factor BCL-2 gene expressions during Chandipura and Dengue virus infections
作者： Dibyakanti Mandal
摘要中文翻译替代剪接(AS)的调控在控制细胞功能方面具有重要意义。超过95%的人类基因会发生替代剪接,而AS由接近100种剪接因子调控。已有证据表明,病毒感染会改变大量宿主基因的剪接模式。本研究探讨了Chandipura病毒(CHPV)对宿主细胞SR蛋白基因表达的影响,并确定这些变化是否会影响凋亡因子BCL-2的水平。CHPV是一种负链RNA病毒,会导致年轻儿童出现发热、脑炎和死亡,其病死率高达50%。过去对宿主因子在CHPV感染中的作用了解甚少,且尚未有研究探讨病毒感染引起的剪接变化。我们的研究结果显示,在CHPV感染的Vero E6细胞中,SR蛋白SRp55和SRp75的表达水平显著下调。为了进行比较,我们还检测了登革病毒(DENV)感染细胞中SRp55和SRp75的水平。结果显示,在DENV感染的细胞中,SRp75水平未发生变化,而SRp55水平升高。我们还确定了凋亡基因BCL-2的大剪接变体和小剪接变体的水平,以探讨改变的剪接因子表达与病毒感染细胞凋亡之间的可能关联。我们的数据表明,在CHPV感染的细胞中,BCL-2大剪接变体的表达水平低于未感染细胞。这是第一项关于CHPV介导的剪接因子表达变化的研究,这些结果提示需要进一步研究其他病毒,以更好地理解病毒感染期间宿主剪接事件的全局变化、细胞存活以及免疫应答。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨Chandipura病毒(CHPV)如何影响宿主细胞SR蛋白的表达,以及这些变化是否会影响凋亡因子BCL-2的水平。背景强调了替代剪接调控在控制细胞功能中的重要性,以及剪接因子在此过程中的作用。
设计与对象
本研究在感染了Chandipura病毒的Vero E6细胞上进行,并将其与未感染细胞进行比较。同时,还与感染了登革病毒(DENV)的细胞进行了比较,以评估SR蛋白表达水平的差异。
核心方法
研究人员分析了CHPV感染的Vero E6细胞中SR蛋白SRp55和SRp75的表达水平,并将其与未感染细胞进行了比较。他们还检测了这些细胞中凋亡基因BCL-2的大剪接变体和小剪接变体的水平,以确定其与改变的剪接因子表达之间的可能关联。
主要结果
研究发现,在CHPV感染的Vero E6细胞中,SRp55和SRp75的表达水平显著低于未感染细胞。相比之下,在DENV感染的细胞中,SRp75的水平保持不变,而SRp55的水平升高。此外,在CHPV感染的细胞中,BCL-2大剪接变体的表达水平也降低。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,CHPV感染会导致SRp55和SRp75的下调,这可能影响凋亡通路。这是第一项报道CHPV介导的剪接因子表达变化的研究,为理解该病毒对宿主细胞过程的影响提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为了解CHPV如何改变宿主剪接因子提供了重要信息,并可能有助于阐明其发病机制。它们还强调了进一步研究其他病毒类似效应的必要性,以及这些效应对细胞存活和免疫应答的潜在影响。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于使用Vero E6细胞进行体外分析,这可能无法完全代表人类感染情况。研究基于单一实验,因此其发现的可信度处于中等水平。

Differential modulation of host cell Serine and Arginine-Rich (SR) protein and apoptotic factor BCL-2 gene expressions during Chandipura and Dengue virus infections

Authors: Dibyakanti Mandal
Original abstract (English)
Regulation of alternative splicing (AS) is important in controlling cellular functions. More than 95% human genes are alternatively spliced and AS is regulated by close to 100 splicing factors. Line of evidences suggest that virus infections alter the AS pattern of numerous host genes. In this study we have investigated Chandipura virus (CHPV) induced alteration of host cell SR protein gene expressions and determined if these changes have any effect on apoptotic factor BCL-2 levels,. CHPV is a negative stranded RNA virus which causes fever, brain encephalitis and death among young children with a case-fatality rate of 50%. Few studies were done in past to understand the roles of host factors in CHPV pathogenesis and no study was done on virus induced changes in AS. Our study showed that there is a down-regulation of SR proteins SRp55 and SRp75 expression levels significantly decreased in the CHPV infected Vero E6 cells compared to uninfected cells. For a comparison, we also looked at the levels of SRp55 and SRp75 in dengue virus (DENV) infected cells. Our results showed that SRp75 levels is unchanged whereas SRp55 levels increased in DENV infected cells. We have also determined the levels of large and small splice variants of apoptotic gene BCL-2 to find out possible correlation between the altered splicing factor expressions and virus infected cell death via apoptosis. Our data indicates that in CHPV infected cells, BCL-2 large splice variant expressions decreased as compared to uninfected cells. This is the first study of CHPV mediated alteration of splicing factor expressions and these results warrant further study with other viruses to better understand the global changes in host splicing events, cell survival, host immune response during virus infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate how Chandipura virus (CHPV) affects the expression of host cell SR proteins and whether these changes influence apoptotic factor BCL-2 levels. The background highlights the importance of alternative splicing regulation in controlling cellular functions and the role of splicing factors in this process.
Design and population
The study was conducted on Vero E6 cells infected with Chandipura virus and compared them to uninfected cells. It also included a comparison with dengue virus (DENV) infected cells to assess differences in SR protein expression levels.
Core methods
The researchers analyzed the expression levels of SR proteins SRp55 and SRp75 in CHPV-infected Vero E6 cells and compared them to uninfected cells. They also examined the levels of large and small splice variants of the BCL-2 gene in these cells to determine any correlation with altered splicing factor expressions.
Main results
The study found that SRp55 and SRp75 expression levels were significantly decreased in CHPV-infected Vero E6 cells compared to uninfected cells. In contrast, SRp75 levels remained unchanged while SRp55 levels increased in DENV-infected cells. Additionally, BCL-2 large splice variant expressions decreased in CHPV-infected cells.
Interpretation and novelty
The results indicate that CHPV infection leads to a down-regulation of SRp55 and SRp75, which may affect apoptotic pathways. This is the first study to report CHPV-mediated changes in splicing factor expressions, offering novel insights into the virus's impact on host cell processes.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings provide important information on how CHPV alters host splicing factors and may contribute to understanding its pathogenesis. They also highlight the need for further research to explore similar effects in other viruses and their implications for cell survival and immune response.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro analysis using Vero E6 cells, which may not fully represent human infections. The evidence is based on a single study, suggesting a moderate confidence level in the findings.

抗ZIKV IgG亚类谱型和抗DENV IgG与妊娠期ZIKV感染后新生儿结局的相关性

Anti‐ZIKV IgG Subclass Profiles and Anti‐DENV IgG Are Associated With Neonatal Outcomes Following ZIKV Infection During Pregnancy
作者： Ana Clara Barbosa Antonelli, Giordana Bruna Moreira Peres, Gabriel Henrique Ciriaco Ferreira, João Paulo Fernandes Caixeta Domingos, Jefferson do Carmo Dietz, Caroline Pires Santana, Ana Laura de Sene Amâncio Zara, Marília Dalva Turchi, Simone Gonçalves da Fonseca, I Martins
摘要中文翻译摘要 登革病毒(DENV)是一种通过伊蚊传播的黄病毒。大多数感染个体表现出轻微症状;然而,垂直传播的ZIKV可能导致先天性寨卡综合征(CZS),包括小头畸形、神经系统、听力、眼科和形态学异常。鉴于黄病毒中DENV暴露的高患病率和抗体交叉反应,了解母体体液抗体谱型与ZIKV感染后新生儿结局之间的关系非常重要。在这项探索性研究中,我们评估了母体和新生儿对ZIKV的总IgG、IgG1和IgG3抗体反应,以及对DENV的IgG反应,与母体-新生儿抗体模式和新生儿结局的关系。样本来自巴西的孕妇及其新生儿,包括患有小头畸形和其他临床或影像学异常的妊娠。通过ELISA检测抗ZIKV和抗DENV IgG反应。在个体比较中,患有眼科异常的妊娠中观察到新生儿抗ZIKV IgG3反应较低,但该关联在假发现率调整后不再显著。在患有眼科异常和任何临床或影像学异常的妊娠中,观察到新生儿抗DENV IgG反应较低,且这两种关联在假发现率调整后仍保持显著。这些探索性关联具有假设生成性,但并未确立所测抗体谱型的功能性保护或因果作用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨孕妇和新生儿对寨卡病毒(ZIKV)和登革病毒(DENV)的抗体谱型与妊娠期ZIKV感染后新生儿结局之间的关系。
设计与对象
在巴西进行了一项探索性研究,收集了孕妇及其新生儿的样本,包括患有小头畸形和其他临床或影像学异常的妊娠。
核心方法
通过ELISA检测抗ZIKV和抗DENV IgG反应,以评估母体和新生儿的抗体模式。
主要结果
在患有眼科异常和任何临床或影像学异常的妊娠中,观察到新生儿抗DENV IgG反应较低,且这两种关联在假发现率调整后仍保持显著。
解释与创新
研究结果表明特定抗体谱型与新生儿结局之间可能存在关联,但并未确立因果关系或功能性保护。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可能为未来关于母体抗体在新生儿健康中作用的研究提供信息,并指导针对性诊断或治疗策略的开发。
局限与证据强度
该研究为探索性研究,未能确立因果关系,其发现需要通过更严谨的研究进一步验证。

Anti‐ZIKV IgG Subclass Profiles and Anti‐DENV IgG Are Associated With Neonatal Outcomes Following ZIKV Infection During Pregnancy

Authors: Ana Clara Barbosa Antonelli, Giordana Bruna Moreira Peres, Gabriel Henrique Ciriaco Ferreira, João Paulo Fernandes Caixeta Domingos, Jefferson do Carmo Dietz, Caroline Pires Santana, Ana Laura de Sene Amâncio Zara, Marília Dalva Turchi, Simone Gonçalves da Fonseca, I Martins
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Zika virus (ZIKV) is a flavivirus transmitted by Aedes mosquitoes. Most infected individuals present mild symptoms; however, vertical transmission of ZIKV can lead to Congenital Zika Syndrome (CZS), which includes microcephaly, neurological, hearing, ophthalmological, and morphological abnormalities. Given the high prevalence of dengue virus (DENV) exposure and antibody cross‐reactivity among flaviviruses, understanding how maternal humoral antibody profiles relate to neonatal outcomes after ZIKV infection is important. In this exploratory study, we evaluated maternal and neonatal anti‐ZIKV total IgG, anti‐ZIKV IgG1 and anti‐ZIKV IgG3, and anti‐DENV IgG reactivity in relation to maternal–neonatal antibody patterns and neonatal outcomes. Samples from pregnant women and their newborns were collected in Brazil, including pregnancies with microcephaly and other clinical or imaging abnormalities. Anti‐ZIKV and anti‐DENV IgG reactivity was measured by ELISA. Lower neonatal anti‐ZIKV IgG3 reactivity was observed in pregnancies with ophthalmological abnormalities in the individual comparison, although this association did not remain significant after false discovery rate adjustment. Lower neonatal anti‐DENV IgG reactivity was observed in pregnancies with ophthalmological abnormalities and with any clinical or imaging abnormality, and both associations remained significant after false discovery rate adjustment. These exploratory associations are hypothesis‐generating and do not establish functional protection or a causal role for the measured antibody profiles.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore the relationship between maternal and neonatal antibody profiles against Zika virus (ZIKV) and Dengue virus (DENV) and neonatal outcomes following ZIKV infection during pregnancy.
Design and population
An exploratory study was conducted in Brazil, collecting samples from pregnant women and their newborns, including those with microcephaly and other clinical or imaging abnormalities.
Core methods
Anti-ZIKV and anti-DENV IgG reactivity was measured using ELISA to assess maternal and neonatal antibody patterns.
Main results
Lower neonatal anti-DENV IgG reactivity was observed in pregnancies with ophthalmological abnormalities and with any clinical or imaging abnormality, and both associations remained significant after false discovery rate adjustment.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest potential associations between specific antibody profiles and neonatal outcomes, though they do not establish a causal role or functional protection.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results may inform future studies on the role of maternal antibodies in neonatal health and guide the development of targeted diagnostic or therapeutic strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is exploratory and does not establish causality, with findings needing further validation through more rigorous studies.

巴西北部无症状献血者中虫媒病毒的分子筛查

Molecular screening of arboviruses in asymptomatic blood donors from Northern Brazil
作者： Alexandre Rodrigues Calazans, Vanessa Duarte da Costa, Marisa de Oliveira Ribeiro, Bárbara Araújo Nogueira, Elida M. V. Brandão, Kátia Cristina Silva Nascimento, Luiz Paulo de Brito Oliveira Souza, Priscilla Lopes da Silva Guimarães, Pedro Henrique Cardoso, Alexandre Vicente Frederico
摘要中文翻译背景:由虫媒病毒登革病毒(登革病毒DENV)、寨卡病毒(ZIKV)和基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)引起的无症状感染对血液安全构成潜在风险,因为病毒血症献血者可能传播严重的输血相关感染。方法:使用ChemagicTM PrimeTM平台从5,954个样本池(每个样本池包含六个样本)中提取病毒RNA,作为巴西NAT PLUS系统的一部分,分析来自亚马逊州血液学与输血病学医院基金会(HEMOAM)收集的混合血浆样本,采用RT-qPCR方法使用ZC D-Tipagem分子检测(Bio-Manguinhos)。结果:在连续十八个流行病学周内,共检测到11例CHIKV阳性病例、4例DENV阳性病例,以及在分析的血袋样本中未检测到ZIKV阳性病例。结论:在雨季期间检测到由虫媒病毒传播引起的无症状感染,表明该地区存在持续的隐性病毒传播,并强调了对献血者监测进行监测的重要性。

Molecular screening of arboviruses in asymptomatic blood donors from Northern Brazil

Authors: Alexandre Rodrigues Calazans, Vanessa Duarte da Costa, Marisa de Oliveira Ribeiro, Bárbara Araújo Nogueira, Elida M. V. Brandão, Kátia Cristina Silva Nascimento, Luiz Paulo de Brito Oliveira Souza, Priscilla Lopes da Silva Guimarães, Pedro Henrique Cardoso, Alexandre Vicente Frederico
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Asymptomatic infections caused by the arboviruses dengue virus (DENV), zika virus (ZIKV) and chikungunya virus (CHIKV) pose a hidden risk to blood safety, as viremic donors may contribute to transfusion-transmitted infections with severe outcomes. METHODS: Viral RNA from 5,954 pools (each pool contains six samples) was extracted using the ChemagicTM PrimeTM platform, as part of the Brazilian NAT PLUS system from pooled plasma samples collected at Amazonas Hematology and Hemotherapy Hospital Foundation (HEMOAM) and analyzed by RT-qPCR using the ZC D-Tipagem molecular assay (Bio-Manguinhos). RESULTS: Over eighteen consecutive epidemiological weeks, 11 CHIKV-positive cases were identified, 4 DENV-positive cases, and no ZIKV-positive detections on the blood bags samples analyzed. CONCLUSION: The detection of asymptomatic arboviral infections during the rainy season, when environmental conditions favor mosquito proliferation and arbovirus transmission, suggests ongoing silent viral circulation in the region and reinforces the importance of blood donor surveillance.
View research seven-element analysis

Asymptomatic infections caused by the arboviruses dengue virus (DENV), zika virus (ZIKV) and chikungunya virus (CHIKV) pose a hidden risk to blood safety, as viremic donors may contribute to transfusion-transmitted infections with severe outcomes.

The supplied evidence does not clearly state the study design, setting, or study population.

Main results
Over eighteen consecutive epidemiological weeks, 11 CHIKV-positive cases were identified, 4 DENV-positive cases, and no ZIKV-positive detections on the blood bags samples analyzed.

使用ARIMA和傅里叶-ARIMA模型对新加坡每周登革热病例进行短期预测

Short-Term Forecasting of Weekly Dengue Cases in Singapore Using ARIMA and Fourier-ARIMA Models
作者： Wentao Shan
摘要中文翻译登革热仍然是新加坡反复出现的公共卫生问题,其中每周报告病例的突然增加会对监测、医疗响应和媒介控制规划造成压力。本研究利用2012-W01至2026-W17期间官方发布的每周登革热监测数据,构建了一个短期预测框架。该数据集被重新组织为连续的流行病学周时间序列,总登革热病例通过合并登革热和登革热出血病例来定义。由于原始序列包含暴发尖峰和不稳定的方差,在模型拟合前采用了对数变换。分析过程中应用了ACF/PACF检验、差分、ARIMA搜索、傅里叶-ARIMA比较、残差诊断以及时间序列的训练-测试验证。前673周用于训练,最后75周用于一周期的测试。结果表明,ARIMA(4,1,0)+Fourier(2)在预测准确性、残差充分性和模型简洁性之间提供了最佳平衡。该模型应被理解为一种实用的监测基准,而非对登革热传播的完整解释。

问题与背景

设计与对象
本研究利用2012-W01至2026-W17期间官方发布的每周登革热监测数据,构建了一个短期预测框架。
核心方法
该数据集被重新组织为连续的流行病学周时间序列,总登革热病例通过合并登革热和登革热出血病例来定义。
主要结果
结果表明,ARIMA(4,1,0)+Fourier(2)在预测准确性、残差充分性和模型简洁性之间提供了最佳平衡。
解释与创新
该模型应被理解为一种实用的监测基准,而非对登革热传播的完整解释。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为新加坡登革热暴发的监测和应对策略提供改进依据。
局限与证据强度

Short-Term Forecasting of Weekly Dengue Cases in Singapore Using ARIMA and Fourier-ARIMA Models

Authors: Wentao Shan
Original abstract (English)
Dengue remains a recurring public health issue in Singapore, where sudden weekly increases in reported cases can create pressure on surveillance, healthcare response, and vector-control planning. This study builds a short-term forecasting framework using official weekly dengue surveillance data from 2012-W01 to 2026-W17. The dataset was reorganized into a continuous epidemiological-week time series, and total dengue cases were defined by combining dengue fever and dengue hemorrhagic fever cases. Because the raw series contains outbreak spikes and unstable variance, the log transformation was used before model fitting. The analysis then applied ACF/PACF inspection, differencing, ARIMA search, Fourier-ARIMA comparison, residual diagnostics, and chronological train-test validation. The first 673 weeks were used for training, and the final 75 weeks were used for one-week-ahead testing. Results indicate that ARIMA(4,1,0)+Fourier(2) offers the strongest balance between prediction accuracy, residual adequacy, and model simplicity. The model should be understood as a practical surveillance baseline rather than a complete explanation of dengue transmission.
View research seven-element analysis

Main results

Interpretation and novelty

Limitations and evidence strength

登革病毒与人类血小板的直接相互作用驱动膜重塑、半胱天冬酶-3激活及细胞外囊泡释放

Direct Interaction of Dengue Virus with Human Platelets Drives Membrane Remodeling, Caspase-3 Activation, and Extracellular Vesicle Release
作者： Mayra Silvia Pérez-Flores, Daniel Núñez-Avellaneda, Ivette Martínez‐Vieyra, J. Manuel Hernández‐Hernández, Gloria León-Ávila, Doris Cerecedo, Ma. Isabel Salazar, Ivette Astrid Martínez-Vieyra, José Manuel Hernández-Hernández
摘要中文翻译登革病毒(登革病毒)感染是急性血小板减少症和血小板功能障碍的常见原因,但其潜在的细胞机制尚未完全阐明。本研究探讨了人类血小板在暴露于DENV-2(MOI 0.1)2小时和4小时后,激活动态、形态变化、凋亡及细胞外囊泡(EV)释放的情况。来自健康供体的洗涤血小板(n=5)在CD62P和CD40L表面动员中表现出显著的供体间异质性,且在阳性率或平均荧光强度上无统计学显著变化(p>0.05)。相反,扫描电子显微镜形态学分析显示血小板显著扩展,与假对照相比,在2小时和4小时时赤道直径增加(p 0.05),但DENV-2更倾向于富集中等大小的微囊泡(150–250 nm)。这些结果表明,血小板对DENV-2的反应表现出独特的脱颗粒、形态扩展及半胱天冬酶-3介导的凋亡激活模式,为阐明可能参与登革热诱导血小板减少症的细胞机制提供了新的见解。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革病毒(登革病毒)感染是急性血小板减少症和血小板功能障碍的常见原因,但其潜在的细胞机制尚未完全阐明。
设计与对象
本研究探讨了人类血小板在暴露于DENV-2(MOI 0.1)2小时和4小时后,激活动态、形态变化、凋亡及细胞外囊泡(EV)释放的情况。
核心方法
来自健康供体的洗涤血小板(n=5)在CD62P和CD40L表面动员中表现出显著的供体间异质性,且在阳性率或平均荧光强度上无统计学显著变化(p>0.05)。
主要结果
相反,扫描电子显微镜形态学分析显示血小板显著扩展,与假对照相比,在2小时和4小时时赤道直径增加(p 0.05),但DENV-2更倾向于富集中等大小的微囊泡(150–250 nm)。
解释与创新
这些结果表明,血小板对DENV-2的反应表现出独特的脱颗粒、形态扩展及半胱天冬酶-3介导的凋亡激活模式,为阐明可能参与登革热诱导血小板减少症的细胞机制提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为登革热诱导血小板减少症的细胞机制提供了新的见解,可能有助于未来对血小板功能障碍及其临床意义的研究。
局限与证据强度
本研究受限于样本量较小(n=5)以及使用健康供体的洗涤血小板,这可能无法完全反映DENV-2与血小板在体内的复杂相互作用。证据强度中等。

Direct Interaction of Dengue Virus with Human Platelets Drives Membrane Remodeling, Caspase-3 Activation, and Extracellular Vesicle Release

Authors: Mayra Silvia Pérez-Flores, Daniel Núñez-Avellaneda, Ivette Martínez‐Vieyra, J. Manuel Hernández‐Hernández, Gloria León-Ávila, Doris Cerecedo, Ma. Isabel Salazar, Ivette Astrid Martínez-Vieyra, José Manuel Hernández-Hernández
Original abstract (English)
Dengue virus (DENV) infection is a frequent cause of acute thrombocytopenia and platelet dysfunction, yet underlying cellular mechanisms remain incompletely understood. In this study, we investigated the dynamics of platelet activation, morphological changes, apoptosis, and extracellular vesicle (EV) release in human platelets exposed to DENV-2 (MOI 0.1) for 2 h and 4 h. Washed platelets from healthy donors (n=5) exhibited marked donor-to-donor heterogeneity in CD62P and CD40L surface mobilization, with no statistically significant shifts in positivity or mean fluorescence intensity (p>0.05). Conversely, scanning electron microscopy morphometry demonstrated significant platelet spreading, showing increased equatorial diameter at 2 h and 4 h versus mock controls (p 0.05), but DENV-2 preferentially enriched medium-sized microvesicles (150–250 nm). These findings suggest that platelet responses to DENV-2 exhibit a distinct pattern of degranulation, morphological spreading, and for caspase-3-mediated apoptotic activation, providing new insights into cellular mechanisms potentially contributing of the dengue-induced thrombocytopenia.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue virus (DENV) infection is a frequent cause of acute thrombocytopenia and platelet dysfunction, yet underlying cellular mechanisms remain incompletely understood.
Design and population

Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide new insights into the cellular mechanisms of dengue-induced thrombocytopenia, which could inform future research on platelet dysfunction and its clinical implications.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its small sample size (n=5) and the use of washed platelets from healthy donors, which may not fully represent the in vivo complexity of DENV-2 interactions with platelets. The evidence is moderate.

登革病毒1型、3型和4型人体挑战模型的免疫转录组学特征比较分析

A Comparative Analysis of the Immunotranscriptomic Features of DENV-1, -3, and -4 Human Challenge Models.
作者： Céline S C Hardy, Lisa A Ware, Heather Friberg, Joel V Chua, Kirsten E Lyke, Stephen J Thomas, Adam T Waickman
摘要中文翻译背景:登革病毒(DENV)感染可引起一系列临床症状,从轻度发热性疾病到严重疾病。较高的DENV RNAemia水平与严重疾病相关,尽管这种关系尚未完全理解。在人体感染模型(DHIMs)中,志愿者被实验性感染低减毒的DENV株,为研究感染的早期病毒学、转录组学和免疫学特征提供了宝贵的工具。使用DENV-1、DENV-3和DENV-4进行的DHIM研究已显示出不同的临床特征;然而,RNAemia和血清型对这些挑战模型中不同的转录组学和临床特征的贡献仍不清楚。方法:在本研究中,我们对DHIM-1、DHIM-3和DHIM-4进行了比较分析,以确定感染的转录组学反应的共同和独特特征及其与RNAemia和临床症状的关联。随后,我们将人外周血单核细胞(PBMCs)在体外暴露于不同滴度的DENV-1或DENV-3,并进行了批量RNA测序。结果:在所有DHIM中,我们发现了一组在RNAemia峰值日上调的保守基因,代表了一种与血清型无关的核心免疫应答反应。此外,在一组具有较高RNAemia水平和症状的DHIM-3参与者中,出现了指示细胞质翻译下调的独特基因特征。体外PBMC暴露于DENV显示,保守和独特的基因表达特征随病毒剂量变化而变化,而非血清型。结论:这些数据表明,病毒载量与实验性DENV感染后的转录组学反应和临床症状相关,有助于我们理解登革热的发病机制和免疫性。科学与公共卫生意义:这些发现为理解登革热的发病机制和免疫性提供了见解,可能有助于开发更有效的诊断工具、治疗策略和疫苗。局限性与证据强度:本研究仅基于摘要证据,可能无法全面反映研究结果。由于缺乏详细的实验数据,证据强度中等。

问题与背景
登革病毒(DENV)感染可引起一系列临床症状,从轻度发热性疾病到严重疾病。较高的DENV RNAemia水平与严重疾病相关,尽管这种关系尚未完全理解。
设计与对象
