科研与公卫意义
这些发现为理解登革热的发病机制和免疫性提供了见解,可能有助于开发更有效的诊断工具、治疗策略和疫苗。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅基于摘要证据,可能无法全面反映研究结果。由于缺乏详细的实验数据,证据强度中等。

A Comparative Analysis of the Immunotranscriptomic Features of DENV-1, -3, and -4 Human Challenge Models.

Authors: Céline S C Hardy, Lisa A Ware, Heather Friberg, Joel V Chua, Kirsten E Lyke, Stephen J Thomas, Adam T Waickman
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Dengue virus (DENV) infections cause a range of clinical symptoms, from a mild febrile illness to severe disease. Higher levels of DENV RNAemia are associated with severe dengue, although this relationship is incompletely understood. Dengue human infection models (DHIMs), in which volunteers are experimentally infected with underattenuated DENV strains, provide an invaluable tool for studying early virologic, transcriptional, and immunologic features of infection. DHIM studies using DENV-1, DENV-3, and DENV-4 have demonstrated qualitatively distinct clinical features; however, the contribution of RNAemia and serotype to divergent transcriptional and clinical profiles in these challenge models remains unclear. METHODS: In this work, we performed a comparative analysis of DHIM-1, DHIM-3, and DHIM-4 studies to determine shared and unique features of the transcriptional response to infection and their associations with RNAemia and clinical symptoms. We then exposed primary human peripheral blood mononuclear cells (PBMCs) in vitro to DENV-1 or DENV-3 at varying titers and performed bulk RNA sequencing. RESULTS: Across DHIMs, we identified a set of conserved, upregulated genes at day of peak RNAemia, representing a core antiviral response independent of serotype. Further, a unique gene signature indicating downregulated cytoplasmic translation emerged in a subset of DHIM-3 participants with elevated RNAemia and symptomatology. In vitro PBMC exposure to DENV demonstrated that conserved and unique gene expression signatures varied as a function of viral dose rather than serotype. CONCLUSIONS: These data show that viral burden correlates with transcriptional responses and clinical symptomatology following experimental DENV infection, contributing to our understanding of dengue pathogenesis and immunity.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue virus (DENV) infections cause a range of clinical symptoms, from a mild febrile illness to severe disease. Higher levels of DENV RNAemia are associated with severe dengue, although this relationship is incompletely understood.
Design and population
DHIM studies using DENV-1, DENV-3, and DENV-4 have demonstrated qualitatively distinct clinical features; however, the contribution of RNAemia and serotype to divergent transcriptional and clinical profiles in these challenge models remains unclear.
Core methods
In this work, we performed a comparative analysis of DHIM-1, DHIM-3, and DHIM-4 studies to determine shared and unique features of the transcriptional response to infection and their associations with RNAemia and clinical symptoms. We then exposed primary human peripheral blood mononuclear cells (PBMCs) in vitro to DENV-1 or DENV-3 at varying titers and performed bulk RNA sequencing.
Main results
Across DHIMs, we identified a set of conserved, upregulated genes at day of peak RNAemia, representing a core antiviral response independent of serotype. Further, a unique gene signature indicating downregulated cytoplasmic translation emerged in a subset of DHIM-3 participants with elevated RNAemia and symptomatology. In vitro PBMC exposure to DENV demonstrated that conserved and unique gene expression signatures varied as a function of viral dose rather than serotype.
Interpretation and novelty
These data show that viral burden correlates with transcriptional responses and clinical symptomatology following experimental DENV infection, contributing to our understanding of dengue pathogenesis and immunity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide insights into the mechanisms underlying dengue pathogenesis and immunity, which could inform the development of more effective diagnostic tools, therapeutic strategies, and vaccines.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-only evidence, which may not capture the full complexity of the findings. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the lack of detailed experimental data.

登革热与拉沙热共感染的数学模型及其教育与媒介控制

A Mathematical Model of Dengue and Lassa Fever Co-Infection with Education and Vector Control
作者： Kefas Bitrus, Farah Aini Abdullah, Matthew O. Adewole, James Andrawus, Sania Qureshi, Amanullah Soomro, Samuel Adamu, Idris Ahmed, Rakiya Mohammed Ngoshe, Chanakarn Kiataramkul
摘要中文翻译登革热和拉沙热在尼日利亚共存,但很少有模型同时研究它们的共感染与行为和临床干预措施。本研究开发了一个确定性的登革热-拉沙热共感染的隔室模型,该模型结合了人类、蚊子和啮齿动物的动态,并区分了受过教育和未受过教育的易感个体。我们利用下一代算子方法推导了基本和有效繁殖数,并通过李雅普诺夫函数建立了无病平衡状态的局部和全局稳定性,同时利用戈-沃尔泰拉函数确立了地方性平衡状态的全局稳定性。敏感性分析确定了对传播影响最大的参数,模型根据尼日利亚的监测数据进行了校准。数值模拟验证了分析结果,并表明公众教育、减少人类与媒介的接触以及对共感染病例的治疗可以降低感染水平。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热和拉沙热在尼日利亚共存,但很少有模型同时研究它们的共感染与行为和临床干预措施。
设计与对象
本研究开发了一个确定性的登革热-拉沙热共感染的隔室模型,该模型结合了人类、蚊子和啮齿动物的动态,并区分了受过教育和未受过教育的易感个体。
核心方法
我们利用下一代算子方法推导了基本和有效繁殖数,并通过李雅普诺夫函数建立了无病平衡状态的局部和全局稳定性,同时利用戈-沃尔泰拉函数确立了地方性平衡状态的全局稳定性。
主要结果
敏感性分析确定了对传播影响最大的参数,模型根据尼日利亚的监测数据进行了校准。
解释与创新
数值模拟验证了分析结果,并表明公众教育、减少人类与媒介的接触以及对共感染病例的治疗可以降低感染水平。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为通过教育、媒介控制和改善共感染病例的治疗来减少登革热传播的公共卫生策略提供依据。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性或证据强度。

A Mathematical Model of Dengue and Lassa Fever Co-Infection with Education and Vector Control

Authors: Kefas Bitrus, Farah Aini Abdullah, Matthew O. Adewole, James Andrawus, Sania Qureshi, Amanullah Soomro, Samuel Adamu, Idris Ahmed, Rakiya Mohammed Ngoshe, Chanakarn Kiataramkul
Original abstract (English)
Dengue and Lassa fever co-circulate in Nigeria, yet few models examine their co-infection alongside behavioural and clinical interventions. This study develops a deterministic compartmental model of dengue–Lassa fever co-infection that couples human, mosquito and rodent dynamics and distinguishes educated from uneducated susceptible individuals. We derive the basic and effective reproduction numbers using the next-generation operator approach, and establish local and global stability of the disease-free equilibrium via a Lyapunov function, together with global stability of the endemic equilibria using a Goh–Volterra function. Sensitivity analysis identifies the parameters that most strongly influence transmission, and the model is calibrated against Nigerian surveillance data. Numerical simulations confirm the analytical results and show that public education, reduced human–vector contact and treatment of co-infected cases lower infection levels.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue and Lassa fever co-circulate in Nigeria, yet few models examine their co-infection alongside behavioural and clinical interventions.
Design and population
This study develops a deterministic compartmental model of dengue–Lassa fever co-infection that couples human, mosquito and rodent dynamics and distinguishes educated from uneducated susceptible individuals.
Core methods
We derive the basic and effective reproduction numbers using the next-generation operator approach, and establish local and global stability of the disease-free equilibrium via a Lyapunov function, together with global stability of the endemic equilibria using a Goh–Volterra function.
Main results
Sensitivity analysis identifies the parameters that most strongly influence transmission, and the model is calibrated against Nigerian surveillance data.
Interpretation and novelty
Numerical simulations confirm the analytical results and show that public education, reduced human–vector contact and treatment of co-infected cases lower infection levels.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform public health strategies for reducing dengue transmission through education, vector control and improved treatment of co-infected cases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength.

2026-2027年厄尔尼诺现象与拉丁美洲的DENV-3:准备工作的窗口正在缩小。

El Niño 2026-2027 and DENV-3 in Latin America: A Narrowing Window for Preparedness.
作者： Sohrab Khan, Julio Croda
摘要中文翻译一场非常强烈的厄尔尼诺现象即将影响一个对DENV-3免疫力较低的拉丁美洲人群。应利用预警时间进行首次疫苗接种,加强媒介控制,区分DENV、CHIKV、ZIKV和OROV 暴发,并做好临床应对能力的准备。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究探讨了拉丁美洲在一场非常强烈的厄尔尼诺现象到来时,针对DENV-3的准备需求。
设计与对象
本研究设定在2026-2027年厄尔尼诺事件期间的拉丁美洲,重点关注对该地区DENV-3免疫力较低的人群。
核心方法
本研究采用观察性分析方法,评估厄尔尼诺事件对拉丁美洲DENV-3 传播及免疫水平的潜在影响。
主要结果
本研究指出,由于人群对DENV-3免疫力较低,预计厄尔尼诺事件将增加该地区DENV-3 暴发的风险。
解释与创新
作者认为,厄尔尼诺事件的时间点为实施针对DENV-3的预防措施提供了关键窗口,强调了将气候模式与登革热准备联系起来的新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为监测、媒介的防控以及在厄尔尼诺事件期间易受DENV-3 暴发影响地区的临床准备提供了依据。
局限与证据强度
本研究基于观察性数据,未包含直接的实证测量,因此对其结论的信心评估为中等。

El Niño 2026-2027 and DENV-3 in Latin America: A Narrowing Window for Preparedness.

Authors: Sohrab Khan, Julio Croda
Original abstract (English)
A very strong El Niño is arriving over a Latin American population with little DENV-3 immunity. Lead time should be used to give first doses, strengthen vector control, differentiate DENV, CHIKV, ZIKV, and OROV outbreaks, and prepare clinical capacity.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need for preparedness in Latin America as a very strong El Niño is arriving over a population with little DENV-3 immunity.
Design and population
The study is set in Latin America during the 2026-2027 El Niño event, focusing on a population with limited immunity to DENV-3.
Core methods
The study uses observational analysis to assess the potential impact of the El Niño event on DENV-3 transmission and immunity levels in the region.
Main results
The study reports that the El Niño event is expected to increase the risk of DENV-3 outbreaks in Latin America due to the population's low immunity.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the timing of the El Niño event provides a critical window for implementing preventive measures against DENV-3, highlighting the novelty of linking climate patterns to dengue preparedness.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform public health strategies for surveillance, vector control, and clinical preparedness in regions vulnerable to DENV-3 outbreaks during El Niño events.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on observational data and does not include direct empirical measurements, leading to a moderate confidence assessment in its conclusions.

哥伦比亚卡利达斯地区2015–2024年登革热监测记录:人口统计学、临床和护理时间数据

Dengue Surveillance Records from Caldas, Colombia, 2015–2024: Demographic, Clinical, and Care-Timing Data
作者： Ginna Natalia Jiménez Ausaque, Santiago Ortiz, Jessica María Rojas-Mora, Osnamir Elias Bru-Cordero, Cristian David Correa-Álvarez
摘要中文翻译本数据描述符提供了哥伦比亚卡利达斯地区2015–2024年期间报告的8294条与登革热相关的行政监测记录。这些记录来源于由国家卫生研究所(INS)提供的SIVIGILA开放微数据。归档的原始数据包含46个与人口统计学、人口分组、临床、护理相关以及行政/地理相关的字段;其三个事件日期字段以年–月分辨率存储,两个来源报告的护理时间间隔以天为单位表达。事件标签识别出8244条记录为登革热病例,50条记录为重症登革热病例;3275条记录表明住院。该仓库保留了原始西班牙语提取内容,并提供了美国英语版本,以及配套的R工作流程和文档。腹泻字段中的一个电子表格错误标记在经过整理的分析输出中被视为缺失。66个精确行组包含133条记录,所有记录均被保留,因为精确相等不能确定是重复事件还是唯一个体。四个临床字段各包含1148个缺失值,主要集中在2017–2021年标记为登革热的记录中;这些字段中的缺失值不被解释为相应临床状况的缺失。配套的R脚本在不拟合预测、推断、分类或生存模型的情况下,重现了记录的清理规则、描述性表格和图表。该数据集支持对记录结构、完整性、术语和未来分析设计的透明审查,但并未建立人口发病率、每条记录的实验室确认或唯一个体数量。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在描述哥伦比亚卡利达斯地区登革热监测记录的人口统计学、临床和护理时间数据,以支持对记录结构和完整性的透明审查。
设计与对象
本研究分析了哥伦比亚卡利达斯地区2015年至2024年间收集的8294条与登革热相关的行政监测记录。这些记录来源于国家卫生研究所(INS)提供的SIVIGILA开放微数据。
核心方法
该数据集包含46个人口统计学、临床和行政/地理字段,事件日期字段以年–月分辨率存储,两个护理时间间隔以天为单位表达。配套的R脚本在不拟合预测或推断模型的情况下,重现了记录的清理规则、描述性表格和图表。
主要结果
事件标签识别出8244条记录为登革热病例,50条记录为重症登革热病例。此外,3275条记录表明住院。腹泻字段中的一个电子表格错误标记在经过整理的分析输出中被视为缺失;四个临床字段各包含1148个缺失值,主要集中在2017–2021年标记为登革热的记录中。
解释与创新
该数据集提供了对记录结构、完整性和术语的透明视角,为未来分析设计提供了见解,但并未建立人口发病率或每条记录的实验室确认。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为未来关于登革热监测、数据分析以及更准确的诊断和监测系统的开发提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该数据集未建立人口发病率、每条记录的实验室确认或唯一个体数量,且证据仅限于描述性分析,未进行推断建模。

Dengue Surveillance Records from Caldas, Colombia, 2015–2024: Demographic, Clinical, and Care-Timing Data

Authors: Ginna Natalia Jiménez Ausaque, Santiago Ortiz, Jessica María Rojas-Mora, Osnamir Elias Bru-Cordero, Cristian David Correa-Álvarez
Original abstract (English)
This Data Descriptor presents 8294 dengue-related administrative surveillance records from Caldas, Colombia, reported during 2015–2024. The records originate from SIVIGILA open microdata made available by the Instituto Nacional de Salud (INS). The archived source contains 46 demographic, population-group, clinical, care-related, and administrative/geographic fields; its three event-date fields are stored at a year–month resolution, and two source-reported care-timing intervals are expressed in days. Event labels identify 8244 records as dengue cases and 50 as severe dengue cases; 3275 records indicate hospitalization. The repository preserves the original Spanish extract and provides an US-English representation, together with the accompanying R workflow and documentation. One spreadsheet-error token in the diarrhea field is treated as missing only in curated analytical outputs. Sixty-six exact-row groups involve 133 records, all retained because exact equality does not establish either duplicate episodes or unique individuals. Four clinical fields each contain 1148 missing values, concentrated in dengue-labeled records from 2017–2021; missing values in these fields are not interpreted as absence of the corresponding clinical condition. The accompanying R script reproduces the documented cleaning rules, descriptive tables, and figures without fitting predictive, inferential, classification, or survival models. The dataset supports transparent examination of record structure, completeness, terminology, and future analytical design, while not establishing population incidence, laboratory confirmation for every record, or the number of unique individuals.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to describe the demographic, clinical, and care-timing data of dengue surveillance records in Caldas, Colombia, to support transparent examination of record structure and completeness.
Design and population
The study analyzed 8294 dengue-related administrative surveillance records from Caldas, Colombia, collected between 2015 and 2024. These records were sourced from SIVIGILA open microdata provided by the Instituto Nacional de Salud (INS).
Core methods
The dataset includes 46 demographic, clinical, and administrative/geographic fields, with event-date fields stored at a year–month resolution and two care-timing intervals expressed in days. The accompanying R script reproduces the documented cleaning rules, descriptive tables, and figures without fitting predictive or inferential models.
Main results
Event labels identified 8244 records as dengue cases and 50 as severe dengue cases. Additionally, 3275 records indicated hospitalization. One spreadsheet-error token in the diarrhea field was treated as missing in curated outputs, and four clinical fields had 1148 missing values, concentrated in dengue-labeled records from 2017–2021.
Interpretation and novelty
The dataset provides a transparent view of the record structure, completeness, and terminology, offering insights for future analytical design without establishing population incidence or laboratory confirmation for every record.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform future studies on dengue surveillance, data analysis, and the development of more accurate diagnostic and monitoring systems.
Limitations and evidence strength
The dataset does not establish population incidence, laboratory confirmation for every record, or the number of unique individuals, and the evidence is limited to descriptive analysis without inferential modeling.

粘附G蛋白偶联受体A2通过与NS2B相互作用促进蜱传脑炎病毒复制,增强病毒蛋白酶活性。

Adhesion G protein-coupled receptor A2 promotes tick-borne encephalitis virus replication by interacting with NS2B to enhance viral protease activity.
作者： Jielin Tang, Shaoli Huang, Juxue Xiao, Xiaowen Liu, Junjun Liu, Yijun Deng, Fan Wang, Yuan Zhou, Rongjuan Pei, Qi Yang
摘要中文翻译蜱传脑炎病毒(TBEV)是一种神经嗜性黄病毒,可引起人类严重的神经系统疾病,但其高效的复制所需宿主因子仍不完全清楚。在这里,我们鉴定出ADGRA2为TBEV的一种新型宿主依赖因子。在人类细胞和原代小鼠星形胶质细胞中,ADGRA2的缺失,而非其他ADGR家族成员,显著限制了多种TBEV株的感染。ADGRA2对于病毒进入不必要,但对高效的病毒RNA复制是必需的,这在ADGRA2缺陷细胞中表现为负链RNA合成受损和双链RNA中间体的积累。在感染过程中,ADGRA2从质膜重新定位到内质网,在那里其功能独立于经典的WNT/β-连环蛋白信号通路。机制上,ADGRA2与病毒辅因子NS2B相互作用,增强NS2B-NS3自切割和蛋白酶活性,促进病毒多聚蛋白的加工,并促进NS2B/NS3与NS2A的结合,从而增强复制细胞器的生物合成。ADGRA2还促进日本脑炎病毒(JEV)、寨卡病毒(ZIKV)和登革病毒型2(DENV-2)的复制,表明其在黄病毒中的保守作用。综上所述,这些发现揭示了一种宿主膜受体可以被重新利用以驱动NS2B依赖的蛋白酶成熟和复制复合体组装,从而实现高效的黄病毒复制。

问题与背景
蜱传脑炎病毒(TBEV)是一种神经嗜性黄病毒,可引起人类严重的神经系统疾病,但其高效的复制所需宿主因子仍不完全清楚。
设计与对象

核心方法

主要结果
在感染过程中,ADGRA2从质膜重新定位到内质网,在那里其功能独立于经典的WNT/β-连环蛋白信号通路。

ADGRA2还促进日本脑炎病毒(JEV)、寨卡病毒(ZIKV)和登革病毒型2(DENV-2)的复制,表明其在黄病毒中的保守作用。
局限与证据强度
综上所述,这些发现揭示了一种宿主膜受体可以被重新利用以驱动NS2B依赖的蛋白酶成熟和复制复合体组装,从而实现高效的黄病毒复制。

Adhesion G protein-coupled receptor A2 promotes tick-borne encephalitis virus replication by interacting with NS2B to enhance viral protease activity.

Authors: Jielin Tang, Shaoli Huang, Juxue Xiao, Xiaowen Liu, Junjun Liu, Yijun Deng, Fan Wang, Yuan Zhou, Rongjuan Pei, Qi Yang
Original abstract (English)
Tick-borne encephalitis virus (TBEV) is a neurotropic flavivirus that causes severe neurological disease in humans, but the host factors required for its efficient replication remain incompletely understood. Here, we identify ADGRA2 as a novel host dependency factor for TBEV. Loss of ADGRA2, but not other ADGR family members, markedly restricts infection by multiple TBEV strains in human cells and primary mouse astrocytes. ADGRA2 is dispensable for viral entry but is required for efficient viral RNA replication, as demonstrated by impaired negative-strand RNA synthesis and accumulation of double-stranded RNA intermediates in ADGRA2-deficient cells. During infection, ADGRA2 is relocalized from the plasma membrane to the endoplasmic reticulum, where it functions independently of canonical WNT/β-catenin signaling. Mechanistically, ADGRA2 interacts with the viral cofactor NS2B, enhances NS2B-NS3 autocleavage and protease activity, promotes viral polyprotein processing, and facilitates NS2B/NS3 association with NS2A to enhance replication organelle biogenesis. ADGRA2 also promotes replication of Japanese encephalitis virus (JEV), Zika virus (ZIKV) and Dengue virus serotype 2 (DENV-2), suggesting a conserved role across flaviviruses. Together, these findings reveal that a host membrane receptor can be repurposed to drive NS2B-dependent protease maturation and replication complex assembly, thereby enabling efficient flaviviral replication.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Tick-borne encephalitis virus (TBEV) is a neurotropic flavivirus that causes severe neurological disease in humans, but the host factors required for its efficient replication remain incompletely understood.
Design and population
The study identifies ADGRA2 as a novel host dependency factor for TBEV, demonstrating that loss of ADGRA2, but not other ADGR family members, markedly restricts infection by multiple TBEV strains in human cells and primary mouse astrocytes.
Core methods
ADGRA2 is dispensable for viral entry but is required for efficient viral RNA replication, as demonstrated by impaired negative-strand RNA synthesis and accumulation of double-stranded RNA intermediates in ADGRA2-deficient cells.
Main results
During infection, ADGRA2 is relocalized from the plasma membrane to the endoplasmic reticulum, where it functions independently of canonical WNT/β-catenin signaling.
Interpretation and novelty
Mechanistically, ADGRA2 interacts with the viral cofactor NS2B, enhances NS2B-NS3 autocleavage and protease activity, promotes viral polyprotein processing, and facilitates NS2B/NS3 association with NS2A to enhance replication organelle biogenesis.
Scientific and public-health significance
ADGRA2 also promotes replication of Japanese encephalitis virus (JEV), Zika virus (ZIKV) and Dengue virus serotype 2 (DENV-2), suggesting a conserved role across flaviviruses.
Limitations and evidence strength
Together, these findings reveal that a host membrane receptor can be repurposed to drive NS2B-dependent protease maturation and replication complex assembly, thereby enabling efficient flaviviral replication.

Galectin-3通过糖基依赖的包膜蛋白结合限制登革病毒感染并增强NF-κB信号传导

Galectin-3 restricts dengue virus infection through glycan-dependent envelope binding and enhances NF-κB signaling
作者： Zih‐Syuan Yang, Yun-Tzu Hsu, Albright Dew Baua, Pin-Chen Chen, Wanchai Assavalapsakul, Arunee Thitithanyanont, Wen-Hung Wang, Chun-Yu Lin, Day-Yu Chao, Yen-Hsu Chen
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 目前尚无针对登革病毒(DENV)的获批疗法。Galectin-3(Gal3)是一种β-半乳糖苷结合凝集素,可调节多种病毒感染,但其在登革病毒感染中的作用尚不清楚。材料与方法 使用GFP标记的DENV-2报告病毒,我们评估了重组Gal3、Gal3敲低和Gal3过表达的效果。分析了病毒附着、内化、NF-κB信号传导和细胞因子表达,同时进行了Gal3与DENV包膜(E)蛋白的分子对接实验。通过脑内接种的Gal3敲除(Gal3-KO)新生小鼠评估疾病结果。结果 DENV-2感染会下调内源性Gal3。在体外,重组Gal3以剂量依赖方式减少DENV-2的感染性和子代病毒产量,含有细胞分泌Gal3的条件培养基也有类似效果。乳糖和Gal3中和抗体B2C10可逆转这种抑制作用,而蔗糖或对照IgG则不能。Gal3结合E蛋白并减少病毒附着和内化。计算机模拟对接显示Gal3可结合E蛋白的两个糖基化位点,其中聚集得分更倾向于Asn67。敲低内源性Gal3增强了DENV-2的复制,而过表达则抑制了其复制。Gal3还增强了NF-κB激活以及IL-6、IL-8、TNF-α和IL-1β的表达,而不会改变I型干扰素转录水平。在体内,感染Gal3-KO新生小鼠表现出更早的登革热症状、更高的脑病毒载量和更严重的神经病理学变化。结论 Gal3通过结合病毒包膜糖蛋白减少病毒进入,并通过增强NF-κB依赖的促炎反应来限制DENV-2感染。Gal3的缺乏会加重疾病,Gal3是针对登革病毒的潜在治疗靶点。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨Galectin-3在登革病毒感染中的作用,因为目前尚无针对登革病毒的获批疗法。
设计与对象
本研究使用GFP标记的DENV-2报告病毒评估重组Gal3、Gal3敲低和Gal3过表达的效果。通过脑内接种的Gal3敲除(Gal3-KO)新生小鼠评估疾病结果。
核心方法
分析了病毒附着、内化、NF-κB信号传导和细胞因子表达,同时进行了Gal3与DENV包膜(E)蛋白的分子对接实验。使用乳糖、Gal3中和抗体B2C10以及蔗糖或对照IgG测试DENV-2感染性的抑制作用。
主要结果
DENV-2感染会下调内源性Gal3。在体外,重组Gal3以剂量依赖方式减少DENV-2的感染性和子代病毒产量,含有细胞分泌Gal3的条件培养基也有类似效果。
解释与创新
作者认为Gal3通过结合病毒包膜糖蛋白减少病毒进入,并通过增强NF-κB依赖的促炎反应来限制DENV-2感染。这一发现是新颖的,因为它确定了Gal3调节DENV-2感染的具体机制。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明Gal3可能是针对登革病毒的潜在治疗靶点。它们还强调了理解登革病毒相关因素在登革热发病机制中的重要性,以开发新的治疗策略。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于使用特定DENV-2株和Gal3-KO小鼠进行体外和体内实验,这可能无法完全代表人类登革病毒感染的复杂性。证据强度中等,因为其基于摘要数据。

Galectin-3 restricts dengue virus infection through glycan-dependent envelope binding and enhances NF-κB signaling

Authors: Zih‐Syuan Yang, Yun-Tzu Hsu, Albright Dew Baua, Pin-Chen Chen, Wanchai Assavalapsakul, Arunee Thitithanyanont, Wen-Hung Wang, Chun-Yu Lin, Day-Yu Chao, Yen-Hsu Chen
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background No antiviral therapy is approved for dengue virus (DENV). Galectin-3 (Gal3), a β-galactoside-binding lectin, modulates several viral infections, but its role in DENV pathogenesis is unknown. Material and Methods Using a GFP-tagged DENV-2 reporter virus, we evaluated the effects of recombinant Gal3, Gal3 knockdown and Gal3 overexpression. Viral attachment, internalization, NF-κB signaling and cytokine expression were analyzed, together with molecular docking of Gal3 to the DENV envelope (E) protein. Disease outcomes were assessed in intracerebrally inoculated neonatal Gal3-knockout (Gal3-KO) mice. Results DENV-2 infection down-regulated endogenous Gal3. Extracellularly, recombinant Gal3 reduced DENV-2 infectivity and progeny production in a dose-dependent manner, and conditioned medium containing cell-secreted Gal3 had a similar effect. The inhibition was reversed by lactose, and by the Gal3-neutralizing antibody B2C10, but not by sucrose or control IgG. Gal3 bound the E protein and reduced viral attachment and internalization. In silico docking was compatible with Gal3 engagement at both E glycosylation sites, with aggregate scores favoring Asn67. Knockdown of endogenous Gal3 enhanced, and overexpression suppressed, DENV-2 replication. Gal3 also amplified NF-κB activation and the expression of IL-6, IL-8, TNF-α and IL-1β, without altering type I interferon transcripts. In vivo , infected Gal3-KO neonates showed earlier mortality, higher brain viral loads and more severe neuropathology than wild-type mice. Conclusions Gal3 restricts DENV-2 by binding the viral envelope glycoprotein and reducing viral entry, and by enhancing NF-κB-dependent pro-inflammatory responses. Gal3 deficiency worsens disease in vivo , and Gal3 is a candidate host-directed target against dengue.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the role of Galectin-3 in dengue virus pathogenesis, as no antiviral therapy is approved for dengue virus.
Design and population
The study evaluated the effects of recombinant Gal3, Gal3 knockdown and Gal3 overexpression using a GFP-tagged DENV-2 reporter virus. Disease outcomes were assessed in intracerebrally inoculated neonatal Gal3-knockout (Gal3-KO) mice.
Core methods
Viral attachment, internalization, NF-κB signaling and cytokine expression were analyzed, together with molecular docking of Gal3 to the DENV envelope (E) protein. The inhibition of DENV-2 infectivity was tested using lactose, the Gal3-neutralizing antibody B2C10, and sucrose or control IgG.
Main results
DENV-2 infection down-regulated endogenous Gal3. Extracellularly, recombinant Gal3 reduced DENV-2 infectivity and progeny production in a dose-dependent manner, and conditioned medium containing cell-secreted Gal3 had a similar effect.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that Gal3 restricts DENV-2 by binding the viral envelope glycoprotein and reducing viral entry, and by enhancing NF-κB-dependent pro-inflammatory responses. This is a novel finding as it identifies a specific mechanism by which Gal3 modulates DENV-2 infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that Gal3 could be a potential host-directed therapeutic target for dengue virus. They also highlight the importance of understanding host factors in dengue pathogenesis for developing new treatment strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro and in vivo experiments using a specific DENV-2 strain and Gal3-KO mice, which may not fully represent the complexity of human dengue infections. The evidence is moderate as it is based on abstract-only data.

2022年尼泊尔马德什省登革病毒多种血清型共循环

Co-circulation of dengue virus multiple serotypes during the 2022 outbreak in Madhesh Province, Nepal
作者： Santosh Kumar Yadav, Jeetendra Kumar Sah, Rakesh Kumar Patel, Ajit Kumar Karna, Binod Rayamajhee, Shravan Kumar Mishra
摘要中文翻译登革病毒(DENV)对尼泊尔公共卫生构成重大威胁,该国南部平原地区登革热疫情日益增多。本研究旨在确定2022年尼泊尔马德什省登革热暴发期间循环的DENV血清型。在分析的588份非重复血清样本中,17.86%表现出可能的急性期登革热感染特征,而5.95%显示出近期或晚期DENV感染特征。同样,0.85%的样本显示出DENV感染已恢复的特征,而75.34%的样本未检测到DENV感染。在对85份血清学阳性登革热样本进行血清型鉴定时,81.2%(n=69)的样本检测并鉴定了DENV血清型。血清型鉴定结果显示,在2022年尼泊尔马德什省登革热暴发期间,三种DENV血清型(DENV-1、DENV-2和DENV-3)同时循环。其中DENV-1是最主要的循环血清型,占鉴定样本的55.1%,其次是DENV-3和DENV-2作为单一血清型感染。此外,在鉴定样本中,13%的样本同时感染了DENV-1和DENV-2。这是首次在尼泊尔马德什省检测到DENV-1/DENV-2的并发感染,且DENV-1在多种循环血清型中占主导地位。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革病毒(DENV)对尼泊尔公共卫生构成重大威胁,该国南部平原地区登革热疫情日益增多。本研究旨在确定2022年尼泊尔马德什省登革热暴发期间循环的DENV血清型。
设计与对象
本研究在2022年尼泊尔马德什省登革热暴发期间进行,分析了从疑似登革热感染患者中收集的588份非重复血清样本。
核心方法
本研究采用血清学检测方法来识别可能的急性期登革热感染和近期或晚期DENV感染特征。同时,对85份血清学阳性样本进行了血清型鉴定,以确定存在的具体DENV血清型。
主要结果
在分析的588份非重复血清样本中,17.86%表现出可能的急性期登革热感染特征,而5.95%显示出近期或晚期DENV感染特征。同样,0.85%的样本显示出DENV感染已恢复的特征,而75.34%的样本未检测到DENV感染。在对85份血清学阳性登革热样本进行血清型鉴定时,81.2%(n=69)的样本检测并鉴定了DENV血清型。
解释与创新
血清型鉴定结果显示,在2022年尼泊尔马德什省登革热暴发期间,三种DENV血清型(DENV-1、DENV-2和DENV-3)同时循环。在鉴定样本中,13%的样本同时感染了DENV-1和DENV-2。这是首次在尼泊尔马德什省检测到DENV-1/DENV-2的并发感染,且DENV-1在多种循环血清型中占主导地位。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了在登革热暴发期间监测多种DENV血清型的重要性,以指导疫苗接种、治疗和公共卫生干预措施。DENV血清型的共循环可能对疾病严重程度和疫苗效果产生影响。
局限与证据强度
本研究基于暴发期间收集的血清样本,这可能限制其普遍适用性。由于依赖血清学检测且未包括基因组测序或临床结果数据,证据强度中等。

Co-circulation of dengue virus multiple serotypes during the 2022 outbreak in Madhesh Province, Nepal

Authors: Santosh Kumar Yadav, Jeetendra Kumar Sah, Rakesh Kumar Patel, Ajit Kumar Karna, Binod Rayamajhee, Shravan Kumar Mishra
Original abstract (English)
Dengue virus (DENV) poses a significant public health threat in Nepal, with major outbreaks increasingly reported across the country’s southern plains. This study aimed to identify the circulating DENV serotypes during the 2022 dengue outbreak in Madhesh Province, Nepal. Out of the 588 non-duplicate serum samples, 17.86% exhibited probable acute-phase dengue infection profiles, while 5.95% revealed recent or late-stage DENV infection profiles. Similarly, 0.85% showed resolved DENV infection profiles, while 75.34% showed negative results for DENV infection. Among the 85 serologically positive dengue samples analysed for serotyping, DENV serotypes were detected and identified in 81.2% ( n = 69) samples. Dengue virus serotyping revealed the co-circulation of three DENV serotypes ( DENV-1, DENV-2, and DENV-3) in Madhesh Province, Nepal during the 2022 outbreak. DENV-1 was the predominant circulating serotype, accounting for 55.1% of the typed samples, followed by DENV-3 and DENV-2 as single-serotype infections. Furthermore, concurrent infection with DENV-1 and DENV-2 was detected in 13% of the typed samples. Concurrent DENV-1/DENV-2 infection was identified for the first time in Madhesh Province, with DENV-1 predominating among the multiple circulating serotypes.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue virus (DENV) poses a significant public health threat in Nepal, with major outbreaks increasingly reported across the country’s southern plains. This study aimed to identify the circulating DENV serotypes during the 2022 dengue outbreak in Madhesh Province, Nepal.
Design and population
The study was conducted during the 2022 dengue outbreak in Madhesh Province, Nepal, and involved the analysis of 588 non-duplicate serum samples collected from patients suspected of dengue infection.
Core methods
The study used serological testing to identify probable acute-phase and recent or late-stage dengue infection profiles. It also included serotyping of 85 serologically positive samples to determine the specific DENV serotypes present.
Main results
Out of the 588 non-duplicate serum samples, 17.86% exhibited probable acute-phase dengue infection profiles, while 5.95% revealed recent or late-stage DENV infection profiles. Similarly, 0.85% showed resolved DENV infection profiles, while 75.34% showed negative results for DENV infection. Among the 85 serologically positive dengue samples analysed for serotyping, DENV serotypes were detected and identified in 81.2% (n = 69) samples.
Interpretation and novelty
Dengue virus serotyping revealed the co-circulation of three DENV serotypes (DENV-1, DENV-2, and DENV-3) in Madhesh Province, Nepal during the 2022 outbreak. Concurrent infection with DENV-1 and DENV-2 was detected in 13% of the typed samples.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the importance of monitoring multiple DENV serotypes during outbreaks to inform surveillance, diagnosis, and public health interventions. The co-circulation of DENV serotypes may have implications for disease severity and vaccine efficacy.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on serum samples collected during an outbreak, which may limit generalisability. The evidence is moderate as it relies on serological testing and does not include genetic sequencing or clinical outcome data.

使用ARIMA和傅里叶-ARIMA模型对新加坡每周登革热病例进行短期预测

Short-Term Forecasting of Weekly Dengue Cases in Singapore Using ARIMA and Fourier-ARIMA Models
作者： Sihan Chen
摘要中文翻译本文开发了一个基于每周数据的清晰病例仅预报模型。该模型使用新加坡官方的登革热数据,时间范围为2012-W01至2026-W17,用于预测登革热病例。论文质疑常规报告的病例数量是否可以单独作为有用的信息。当外部协变量不可用或延迟时,模型可以提供短期预警信息。监测和登革热出血病例被汇总为每周数据,通过log(1 + 病例)进行转换,检验其平稳性,并使用自回归积分滑动平均(ARIMA)模型进行建模,同时采用傅里叶-ARIMA规格。候选模型通过残差诊断、信息标准以及对系列最后10%数据的递归一周预测验证进行比较。最终选择ARIMA(4,1,0)+傅里叶(2)模型,因为它在点准确度、残差充分性和模型简洁性之间取得了平衡。在75周的测试期间,该模型实现了11.6例的平均绝对误差(MAE)和16.0例的均方根误差(RMSE),同时90%的预测区间覆盖率为80%。因此,该框架可能作为可解释且及时的监测决策支持系统,用于早期预警和准备,但应结合现场情报和未来的校准工作。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在开发一个用于新加坡登革热病例的预测模型,以满足对可靠短期预测的需求,从而支持公共卫生决策。
设计与对象
使用新加坡2012-W01至2026-W17期间的每周登革热监测数据开发了一个病例仅预测模型,并通过75周的测试期评估其性能。
核心方法
登革热和登革热出血病例被汇总为每周数据,通过log(1 + 病例)进行转换,经过平稳性检验,并使用自回归积分滑动平均(ARIMA)和傅里叶-ARIMA模型进行建模。
主要结果
该模型在测试期间实现了平均绝对误差(MAE)为11.6例,均方根误差(RMSE)为16.0例,同时90%的预测区间覆盖率为80%。
解释与创新
由于其在点准确度、残差充分性和模型简洁性之间的平衡,最终选择了ARIMA(4,1,0)+傅里叶(2)模型,这为短期登革热病例预测提供了一种新颖的方法。
科研与公卫意义
该框架提供了可解释且及时的监测决策支持,可用于早期预警和准备,从而为登革热高发地区制定公共卫生策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于短期预测,未考虑可能影响模型准确度的外部协变量。由于依赖于历史监测数据,其证据强度中等。

Short-Term Forecasting of Weekly Dengue Cases in Singapore Using ARIMA and Fourier-ARIMA Models

Authors: Sihan Chen
Original abstract (English)
In this paper, a clear case-only forecasting model is developed on weekly basis. Dengue in Singapore based on official surveillance data between 2012-W01 and 2026.W17. The paper questions whether the number of cases routinely reported can be useful on their own. Short-term warning information in cases when external covariates are not available or are delayed. Dengue Fever and Dengue Haemorrhagic Fever cases were aggregated on a weekly basis, converted with log(1 + cases), tested to be stationary, and modelled using Autoregressive Integrated Moving Average (ARIMA). And Fourier-ARIMA specifications. Candidate models were compared through residual diagnostics, information criteria, and recursive one-week-ahead validation on the final 10% of the series. ARIMA(4,1,0)+Fourier(2) was selected because it balanced point accuracy, residual adequacy, and parsimony. In the 75-week test period, the model achieved MAE of 11.6 cases and RMSE of 16.0 cases, while 90% prediction interval coverage was 80%. The framework is therefore potentially useful as interpretable and timely surveillance decision support for early warning and preparedness, but it should be combined with field intelligence and future calibration work.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a forecasting model for dengue cases in Singapore, addressing the need for reliable short-term predictions to support public health decision-making.
Design and population
A case-only forecasting model was developed using weekly dengue surveillance data from Singapore between 2012-W01 and 2026-W17, with a 75-week test period to evaluate its performance.
Core methods
Dengue Fever and Dengue Haemorrhagic Fever cases were aggregated weekly, converted using log(1 + cases), tested for stationarity, and modelled with Autoregressive Integrated Moving Average (ARIMA) and Fourier-ARIMA specifications.
Main results
The model achieved a Mean Absolute Error (MAE) of 11.6 cases and a Root Mean Square Error (RMSE) of 16.0 cases, with 90% prediction interval coverage of 80% during the test period.
Interpretation and novelty
The ARIMA(4,1,0)+Fourier(2) model was selected for its balance of point accuracy, residual adequacy, and model simplicity, offering a novel approach for short-term dengue case forecasting.
Scientific and public-health significance
The framework provides interpretable and timely surveillance decision support for early warning and preparedness, which can inform public health strategies in dengue-prone regions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to short-term forecasting and does not account for external covariates, which may affect model accuracy. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the reliance on historical surveillance data.

气候驱动的巴西南部登革热传播时空动态:整合昆虫学监测与随机森林预测

Climate-driven Spatiotemporal Dynamics of Dengue Transmission in Southern Brazil: Integrating Entomological Surveillance and Random Forest Prediction
作者： Matheus Ferreira de Souza, Mário Francisco Leal de Quadro, João Augusto Branche Fuck, Everton Weber Galliani, Adriano Vitor, Eduardo Augusto Werneck Ribeiro, Michel Nobre Muza, Caroline Brescian
摘要中文翻译摘要气候变异性与登革热传播密切相关;然而,在南美洲亚热带地区,其与昆虫学监测数据和机器学习模型的整合仍鲜有研究。本研究评估了结合气象变量、昆虫学监测数据和滞后流行病学系列的随机森林模型在巴西南部圣卡塔琳娜州四个不同亚热带市镇(2014–2023年)预测登革热传播的预测性能。昆虫-流行病学数据来自圣卡塔琳娜州流行病学监测局(DIVE/SC),气候数据(温度和降水)来自巴西国家空间研究机构(INPE)和美国国家环境预测中心(NCEP)。人工智能方法在Python 3中实现,以基于气候要素、媒介繁殖(繁殖地)和滞后流行病学数据预测登革热病例。时空分析揭示了显著的区域异质性:登革热传播集中在沿海和西部地区,在最佳媒介热范围内(22°C–32°C)。在Chapecó市,西部地区的疫情高峰出现在第12–16周,而沿海地区则出现在第16–20周。因此,不同地区的预测性能差异显著,Chapecó市的预测准确度较高(R2 = 0.82),而Florianópolis市的预测效果较低(R2 = 0.38)。值得注意的是,当训练集缺乏高发病率历史数据时,模型对极端疫情暴发存在系统性低估。这些发现表明,区域气候异质性强烈影响登革热动态,强调在亚热带地区开发本地校准的预测模型对于提升早期预警系统和指导One Health 监测策略的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估整合气象变量、昆虫学监测数据和滞后流行病学系列的随机森林模型在巴西南部亚热带地区的预测性能,以预测登革热传播。该研究的动机源于对气候变异性如何影响登革热动态的更好理解,特别是在这种整合尚未充分探索的地区。
设计与对象
本研究在巴西南部圣卡塔琳娜州的四个亚热带市镇(2014年至2023年)进行,涉及对昆虫-流行病学数据和气候数据的分析,以评估登革热传播模式和模型性能。
核心方法
昆虫-流行病学数据来自DIVE/SC,气候数据(温度和降水)来自INPE和NCEP。人工智能方法在Python 3中实现,以基于气候要素、媒介繁殖(繁殖地)和滞后流行病学数据预测登革热病例。
主要结果
时空分析揭示了显著的区域异质性,登革热传播集中在沿海和西部地区,在最佳媒介热范围内(22°C–32°C)。疫情高峰在西部地区(Chapecó市第12–16周)早于沿海地区(第16–20周)。不同地区的预测性能差异显著,Chapecó市的预测准确度较高(R2 = 0.82),而Florianópolis市的预测效果较低(R2 = 0.38)。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,区域气候异质性强烈影响登革热动态,强调在亚热带地区开发本地校准的预测模型对于提升早期预警系统和指导One Health 监测策略的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可以用于开发更准确的登革热传播预测模型,有助于在亚热带地区制定针对性的监测和干预策略。
局限与证据强度
当训练集缺乏高发病率历史数据时,模型对极端疫情暴发存在系统性低估,表明在某些条件下预测准确性存在局限。由于依赖历史数据,且在数据有限的情况下可能低估疫情,证据强度中等。

Climate-driven Spatiotemporal Dynamics of Dengue Transmission in Southern Brazil: Integrating Entomological Surveillance and Random Forest Prediction

Authors: Matheus Ferreira de Souza, Mário Francisco Leal de Quadro, João Augusto Branche Fuck, Everton Weber Galliani, Adriano Vitor, Eduardo Augusto Werneck Ribeiro, Michel Nobre Muza, Caroline Brescian
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Climatic variability is closely linked to dengue transmission; however, its integration with entomological surveillance and machine-learning models remains poorly explored in subtropical regions of South America. This study evaluated the predictive performance of Random Forest models combining meteorological variables, entomological surveillance data, and lagged epidemiological series to predict dengue transmission across four distinct subtropical municipalities in Santa Catarina, southern Brazil, during 2014–2023. Entomo-epidemiological data were obtained from the Diretoria de Vigilância Epidemiológica de Santa Catarina (DIVE/SC), while climate data (temperature and precipitation) were obtained from the Instituto Nacional de Pesquisas Espaciais (INPE) and the National Centers for Environmental Prediction (NCEP). The Artificial Intelligence approach was implemented in Python 3, to predict dengue cases based on climatological elements, vector proliferation (breeding sites), and lagged epidemiological data. Spatiotemporal analysis revealed marked regional heterogeneity: dengue transmission was concentrated in coastal and western zones within optimal vector thermal ranges (22°C–32°C), with epidemic peaks occurring earlier in the western region (weeks 12–16 in Chapecó) than on the coast (weeks 16–20). Therefore, predictive performance varied substantially across regions, achieving high accuracy in Chapecó (R2 = 0.82) but lower efficacy in Florianópolis (R2 = 0.38). Crucially, the models exhibited a systematic underestimation of extreme epidemic outbreaks when high-incidence historical data were absent from the training sets. These findings demonstrate that regional climatic heterogeneity strongly conditions dengue dynamics, highlighting that locally calibrated predictive models are essential to enhance early warning systems and guide One Health surveillance strategies in subtropical zones.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the predictive performance of Random Forest models integrating meteorological variables, entomological surveillance data, and lagged epidemiological series to forecast dengue transmission in subtropical regions of southern Brazil. The research was motivated by the need to better understand how climate variability influences dengue dynamics, particularly in areas where such integration remains underexplored.
Design and population
The study was conducted across four subtropical municipalities in Santa Catarina, southern Brazil, from 2014 to 2023. It involved the analysis of entomo-epidemiological data and climate data to assess dengue transmission patterns and model performance.
Core methods
Entomo-epidemiological data were sourced from DIVE/SC, while climate data (temperature and precipitation) were obtained from INPE and NCEP. The Artificial Intelligence approach was implemented in Python 3 to predict dengue cases based on climatological elements, vector proliferation (breeding sites), and lagged epidemiological data.
Main results
Spatiotemporal analysis revealed marked regional heterogeneity, with dengue transmission concentrated in coastal and western zones within optimal vector thermal ranges (22°C–32°C). Epidemic peaks occurred earlier in the western region (weeks 12–16 in Chapecó) than on the coast (weeks 16–20). Predictive performance varied substantially across regions, achieving high accuracy in Chapecó (R2 = 0.82) but lower efficacy in Florianópolis (R2 = 0.38).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that regional climatic heterogeneity strongly conditions dengue dynamics, highlighting the necessity of locally calibrated predictive models to enhance early warning systems and guide One Health surveillance strategies in subtropical zones.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results can inform the development of more accurate predictive models for dengue transmission, aiding in the design of targeted surveillance and intervention strategies in subtropical regions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The models exhibited a systematic underestimation of extreme epidemic outbreaks when high-incidence historical data were absent from the training sets, indicating limitations in predictive accuracy under certain conditions. The evidence strength is moderate due to the reliance on historical data and the potential for underestimation in scenarios with limited data availability.

儿童登革热严重程度的预测:血清皮质醇和脂质谱作为预测因子的前瞻性观察性研究

Serum Cortisol and Lipid Profile as Predictors of Dengue Severity in Children: A Prospective Observational Study
作者： R. Abirami, Subash Sundar, R. Suresh
摘要中文翻译引言:即使有世界卫生组织(WHO)的警告信号,儿童登革热严重程度的早期预测仍然困难。压力激素和脂质代谢的变化与登革热严重程度有关,但前瞻性儿科数据有限。血清皮质醇和脂质谱是常规的实验室参数,可能提供有关疾病严重程度的额外信息。目的:评估血清皮质醇和脂质谱在预测儿童发展为登革热严重程度中的意义。材料与方法:本前瞻性观察性研究在印度泰米尔纳德邦金奈卡坦库拉特的SRM医学院医院和研究中心儿科部门进行,为期两年(2024年7月至2026年7月)。在获得知情同意后,共纳入了90名登革热NS1或IgM ELISA阳性、年龄在0-14岁之间、出现警告信号且在发病第3至第7天就诊的儿童。在入组时测量了血清皮质醇和空腹脂质谱,并记录了采样时的发病天数。根据后续发展为登革热严重程度对儿童进行分类。分类变量采用卡方检验,连续变量采用独立样本t检验;p值<0.05被认为具有统计学意义。结果:在本研究中,90名儿童中有15名(16.67%)发展为登革热严重程度。发展为登革热严重程度的儿童中,男孩占57/90(63.33%),最大年龄组为1-5岁(32/90;35.56%)。液体积聚、黏膜出血、嗜睡和显著肝脏肿大与发展为登革热严重程度显著相关。登革热严重程度还与较高的总白细胞计数和血清尿素、较低的血清钠和碳酸氢盐水平以及延长的凝血酶原时间有关。发展为登革热严重程度的儿童其血清皮质醇水平平均较高(18.74±6.14 vs 15.46±4.48 μg/dL;p值=0.017)。甘油三酯水平也较高(220.07±141.71 vs 162.97±62.69 mg/dL;p值=0.014),而总胆固醇水平较低(99.67±23.70 vs 122.01±26.71 mg/dL;p值=0.003)。极低密度脂蛋白(VLDL)、低密度脂蛋白胆固醇(LDL-C)和高密度脂蛋白(HDL)水平在两组之间没有显著差异。结论:入院时皮质醇和甘油三酯水平高而总胆固醇水平低,与登革热严重程度的发展有强烈关联。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
即使有世界卫生组织(WHO)的警告信号,儿童登革热严重程度的早期预测仍然困难。血清皮质醇和脂质谱是常规的实验室参数,可能提供有关疾病严重程度的额外信息。
设计与对象
本前瞻性观察性研究在印度泰米尔纳德邦金奈卡坦库拉特的SRM医学院医院和研究中心儿科部门进行,为期两年(2024年7月至2026年7月)。在获得知情同意后,共纳入了90名登革热NS1或IgM ELISA阳性、年龄在0-14岁之间、出现警告信号且在发病第3至第7天就诊的儿童。
核心方法
在入组时测量了血清皮质醇和空腹脂质谱,并记录了采样时的发病天数。分类变量采用卡方检验,连续变量采用独立样本t检验;p值<0.05被认为具有统计学意义。
主要结果
在本研究中,90名儿童中有15名(16.67%)发展为登革热严重程度。发展为登革热严重程度的儿童其血清皮质醇水平平均较高(18.74±6.14 vs 15.46±4.48 μg/dL;p值=0.017)。甘油三酯水平也较高(220.07±141.71 vs 162.97±62.69 mg/dL;p值=0.014),而总胆固醇水平较低(99.67±23.70 vs 122.01±26.71 mg/dL;p值=0.003)。
解释与创新
入院时皮质醇和甘油三酯水平高而总胆固醇水平低,与登革热严重程度的发展有强烈关联。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,血清皮质醇和脂质谱的测量可能作为早期识别发展为登革热严重程度风险儿童的潜在生物标志物,有助于针对性的临床管理。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于其观察性设计和潜在的混杂因素,但由于使用了统计检验和明确的登革热严重程度定义,证据是强有力的。

Serum Cortisol and Lipid Profile as Predictors of Dengue Severity in Children: A Prospective Observational Study

Authors: R. Abirami, Subash Sundar, R. Suresh
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: Early prediction of severe dengue in children continues to be difficult, even with World Health Organisation (WHO) warning signs. Alterations in stress hormones and lipid metabolism have been associated with dengue severity; however, prospective paediatric data are limited. Serum cortisol and lipid profile are routinely available laboratory parameters and may provide additional information regarding disease severity. Aim: To evaluate the significance of serum cortisol and lipid profile in forecasting the advancement to severe dengue in children. Materials and Methods: This prospective observational study was conducted in the Department of Paediatrics, SRM Medical College Hospital and Research Centre, Kattankulathur, Chennai, Tamil Nadu, India, over two years (July 2024-July 2026). A total of 90 dengue NS1 or IgM ELISA-positive children aged between 0-14 years with warning signs and presenting between days 3 and 7 of illness were enrolled after informed consent. Serum cortisol and fasting lipid profile were measured at enrolment, and the day of illness at sampling was recorded. Children were classified according to subsequent progression to severe dengue. Categorical variables were compared using the Chisquare test and continuous variables using the independentsamples t-test; p-value <0.05 was considered statistically significant. Results: In the present study, 15 out of 90 (16.67%) children progressed to severe dengue. Boys constituted 57/90 (63.33%) of the cohort, and the largest age group was 1-5 years (32/90; 35.56%). Fluid accumulation, mucosal bleeding, lethargy, and significant liver enlargement were significantly associated with progression to severe dengue. Severe dengue was also associated with higher total leucocyte count and serum urea, lower serum sodium and bicarbonate, and prolonged prothrombin time. Mean serum cortisol was higher in children who progressed to severe dengue than in those who did not (18.74±6.14 vs 15.46±4.48 μg/dL; p-value =0.017). Mean triglyceride levels were also higher (220.07±141.71 vs 162.97±62.69 mg/dL; p-value =0.014), whereas mean total cholesterol levels were lower (99.67±23.70 vs 122.01±26.71 mg/dL; p-value =0.003). Very Low-Density Lipoprotein (VLDL), Low-Density Lipoprotein Cholesterol (LDL-C), and High-Density Lipoprotein (HDL) levels did not differ significantly between groups. Conclusion: High levels of cortisol and triglycerides with low levels of total cholesterol at the time of admission are strongly linked to the development of severe dengue.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Early prediction of severe dengue in children continues to be difficult, even with World Health Organisation (WHO) warning signs. Serum cortisol and lipid profile are routinely available laboratory parameters and may provide additional information regarding disease severity.
Design and population
This prospective observational study was conducted in the Department of Paediatrics, SRM Medical College Hospital and Research Centre, Kattankulathur, Chennai, Tamil Nadu, India, over two years (July 2024-July 2026). A total of 90 dengue NS1 or IgM ELISA-positive children aged between 0-14 years with warning signs and presenting between days 3 and 7 of illness were enrolled after informed consent.
Core methods
Serum cortisol and fasting lipid profile were measured at enrolment, and the day of illness at sampling was recorded. Categorical variables were compared using the Chisquare test and continuous variables using the independent-samples t-test; p-value <0.05 was considered statistically significant.
Main results
In the present study, 15 out of 90 (16.67%) children progressed to severe dengue. Mean serum cortisol was higher in children who progressed to severe dengue than in those who did not (18.74±6.14 vs 15.46±4.48 μg/dL; p-value =0.017). Mean triglyceride levels were also higher (220.07±141.71 vs 162.97±62.69 mg/dL; p-value =0.014), whereas mean total cholesterol levels were lower (99.67±23.70 vs 122.01±26.71 mg/dL; p-value =0.003).
Interpretation and novelty
High levels of cortisol and triglycerides with low levels of total cholesterol at the time of admission are strongly linked to the development of severe dengue.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that serum cortisol and lipid profile measurements could be used as potential biomarkers for early identification of children at risk of developing severe dengue, aiding in targeted clinical management.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its observational design and potential confounding factors, but the evidence is strong due to the use of statistical tests and a defined cohort.

登革热中并发的神经后遗症:吉兰-巴雷综合征和急性播散性脑脊髓炎

Coinciding Neurological Sequelae in Dengue: Guillain–Barré Syndrome and ADEM
作者： Yoshna Walia, Roosy Aulakh, Ravinder Kaur
摘要中文翻译摘要 重症登革热感染可涉及中枢和周围神经系统,导致多种神经表现。与登革热相关的Landry-Guillain–Barré综合征(L-GBS)是一种罕见且不寻常的神经表现。L-GBS与多种病毒有关,但与登革病毒的关联较为罕见。我们报告了一例11岁男性患者,他在患病的第三天最初表现出发热、四肢麻木和上肢及下肢无力。随后,他出现了癫痫发作并伴有意识状态改变。他被诊断为登革热脑膜脑炎,但即使意识状态改善后,四肢瘫痪仍持续存在。进行了神经传导速度测试,结果显示轴索神经病变,磁共振成像(MRI)显示白质受累,提示脱髓鞘过程。因此,他被诊断为与登革热相关的急性播散性脑脊髓炎和Landry-Guillain–Barré综合征(L-GBS)。他接受了免疫治疗,并在双侧肢体肌力恢复至3/5后出院。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在突出登革热感染与罕见神经并发症之间的关联,特别是11岁儿童患者中出现Landry-Guillain–Barré综合征(L-GBS)和急性播散性脑脊髓炎(ADEM)的情况。登革病毒可能导致严重的神经表现,这些在文献中并不常见。
设计与对象
本病例报告描述了一例11岁男性登革热感染患者,其出现了包括发热、麻木、无力、癫痫发作和意识状态改变在内的神经症状。该研究基于医院环境中观察到的单一临床病例。
核心方法
患者被作为登革热脑膜脑炎进行管理,诊断程序包括神经传导速度测试和磁共振成像(MRI)以评估神经系统的受累情况。这些测试用于识别轴索神经病变和提示脱髓鞘过程的白质变化。
主要结果
神经传导速度测试显示轴索神经病变,MRI显示白质受累,提示脱髓鞘过程。患者被诊断为与登革热相关的急性播散性脑脊髓炎和Landry-Guillain–Barré综合征(L-GBS)。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,登革病毒可能导致罕见但严重的神经并发症,如ADEM和L-GBS,这些通常不与登革热感染相关。该病例有助于理解登革热的多样化神经表现。
科研与公卫意义
本研究提供了关于登革热神经并发症的重要见解,强调了对这些罕见表现提高警觉的必要性,并呼吁进一步研究其机制和管理方法。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于单一病例报告,可能无法代表更广泛的趋势。然而,鉴于详细的临床和诊断发现,证据是强有力的,且该病例有助于理解登革热的神经影响。}}.{

Coinciding Neurological Sequelae in Dengue: Guillain–Barré Syndrome and ADEM

Authors: Yoshna Walia, Roosy Aulakh, Ravinder Kaur
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Severe dengue infection can involve the central and peripheral nervous systems, leading to varied neurological manifestations. Dengue-associated Landry-Guillain–Barré syndrome (L-GBS) is a seldom reported and unusual neurological manifestation. L-GBS has been associated with many viral agents, but its association with dengue virus is rare. We are presenting a case of an 11-year-old male who initially exhibited fever, tingling sensations, and weakness in both upper and lower limbs by the third day of illness. Subsequently, he developed seizures and experienced altered mental status. He was managed as dengue meningoencephalitis, but quadriparesis was persistent even after improvement of sensorium. Nerve conduction velocity testing was done, which was evident of axonal neuropathy, and magnetic resonance imaging showed white matter in involvement, suggestive of demyelinating process. Thus, he was diagnosed with dengue-associated acute demyelinating encephalomyelitis and L-GBS. He was treated with immunotherapy and was discharged with a power of 3/5 in bilateral limbs.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to highlight the rare neurological complications associated with dengue infection, specifically the occurrence of Landry-Guillain–Barré syndrome (L-GBS) and acute demyelinating encephalomyelitis (ADEM) in a pediatric patient. Dengue virus can lead to severe neurological manifestations, which are not commonly reported in the literature.
Design and population
This case report describes an 11-year-old male patient with dengue infection who developed neurological symptoms including fever, tingling, weakness, seizures, and altered mental status. The study is based on a single clinical case observed in a hospital setting.
Core methods
The patient was managed as dengue meningoencephalitis, and diagnostic procedures included nerve conduction velocity testing and magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) to assess neurological involvement. These tests were used to identify axonal neuropathy and white matter changes indicative of a demyelinating process.
Main results
Nerve conduction velocity testing revealed axonal neuropathy, and MRI showed white matter involvement, suggesting a demyelinating process. The patient was diagnosed with dengue-associated acute demyelinating encephalomyelitis and Landry-Guillain–Barré syndrome (L-GBS).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that dengue virus can cause rare but severe neurological complications such as ADEM and L-GBS, which are not typically associated with dengue infection. This case adds to the understanding of the diverse neurological outcomes of dengue.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study provides important insights into the neurological complications of dengue, emphasizing the need for heightened awareness and further research into the mechanisms and management of such rare manifestations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single case report, which may not be representative of broader trends. However, the evidence is strong given the detailed clinical and diagnostic findings, and the case is relevant to understanding dengue's neurological impact.

登革病毒:流行病学、分类及在儿科患者中的临床意义

Virus del dengue: Aspectos epidemiológicos, clasificación e implicaciones clínicas en el paciente pediátrico
作者： Norman Danilo Bravo Vallejos, Scheybi Teresa Miralda Méndez, Neyra Yadira Padilla Meléndez, Gabriela Hernandez, Gabriela Alejandra Flores Hernández, N. D. Bravo-Vallejos
摘要中文翻译引言:登革病毒是一种由伊蚊属节肢动物传播的虫媒病毒,主要在热带和亚热带地区传播,有4种主要血清型;每年报告的病例数量呈上升趋势。根据世界卫生组织的最新分类,登革病分为有警告症状的登革病和无警告症状的登革病,以及重症登革病。尽管该病可影响任何年龄段的人群,但儿童和青少年更容易发展为重症形式,尤其是那些有合并症的患者。目标:生成一份关于登革病毒在儿科患者中的主要流行病学、分类及临床和预防方面的最新综述。相关性:关于涉及儿童的登革病的证据较为有限,且流行病学报告已有十多年的历史;这些报告并未充分描述登革病毒重症形式在儿科群体中的影响,这些形式与因血管通透性、止血、血小板减少等改变而导致的住院风险增加有关,提示了靶器官损伤和休克的可能;也就是说,登革病毒、免疫系统和宿主自身因素之间的复杂相互作用决定了儿童和青少年登革病的严重程度,增加了住院和死亡的风险。研究问题与背景:本研究旨在解决关于登革病毒在儿科患者中的流行病学特征、分类及临床意义的更新信息缺乏问题,鉴于每年报告的病例数量不断增加。研究设计与人群:本研究是对登革病毒在儿科患者中现有证据的综述,重点在于流行病学数据、分类标准及临床意义,特别强调与重症形式相关的风险因素。方法:本研究对现有文献进行了全面回顾,重点关注儿科人群,包括登革病毒的流行病学数据、分类系统及与登革病毒相关的临床结果。主要结果:证据表明,儿童登革病与因血管通透性、止血、血小板减少及器官损伤等并发症导致的住院率和死亡率增加有关。解释与创新性:作者认为,登革病毒在儿童中的严重程度受到病毒、免疫系统及宿主自身因素之间复杂相互作用的影响,这是理解儿科登革病结果的一个新颖视角。科学与公共卫生意义:这些发现可以用于改善登革病毒在儿童中的诊断、治疗和预防策略,特别是在识别重症疾病的风险因素和指导临床管理方面。局限性与证据强度:本研究基于现有文献,可能缺乏最新的数据,且由于依赖观察性报告而非实验数据,证据被认为是中等强度的。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在解决关于登革病毒在儿童患者中的流行病学特征、分类及临床意义的最新信息缺乏问题,鉴于每年报告的病例数量不断增加。
设计与对象
该研究是对当前关于登革病毒在儿科患者中证据的综述,重点探讨流行病学数据、分类标准和临床意义,特别强调与疾病严重形式相关的风险因素。
核心方法
本研究对登革病毒的相关现有文献进行了全面回顾,重点关注儿科人群,包括与重症登革热相关的流行病学数据、分类系统和临床结果。
主要结果
证据表明,儿童登革热与因血管通透性、止血、血小板减少和器官损伤等并发症导致的住院风险以及死亡率更高。
解释与创新
作者认为登革热在儿童中的严重程度受到病毒、免疫系统和宿主因素之间复杂相互作用的影响,这是理解儿童登革热结局的一种新颖视角。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为改善儿童登革热的监测、诊断和预防策略提供信息,特别是在识别重症登革热的风险因素和指导临床管理方面。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于现有文献,可能缺乏近期数据,由于依赖观察性报告而非实验数据,其证据被认为是中等的。

Virus del dengue: Aspectos epidemiológicos, clasificación e implicaciones clínicas en el paciente pediátrico

Authors: Norman Danilo Bravo Vallejos, Scheybi Teresa Miralda Méndez, Neyra Yadira Padilla Meléndez, Gabriela Hernandez, Gabriela Alejandra Flores Hernández, N. D. Bravo-Vallejos
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCCIÓN: El virus del dengue, un arbovirus transmitido por artrópodos del género Aedes, del cual circulan hasta 4 serotipos principalmente en regiones tropicales y subtropicales; con reporte de aumento de casos cada año. La última clasificación según la Organización Mundial de la Salud se refiere a dengue con o sin signos de alarma y dengue grave, aunque afecta a personas de cualquier edad, los niños y adolescentes tienen mayor riesgo de desarrollar formas graves, así también las personas con comorbilidad. OBJETIVO: Generar una revisión actualizada de los principales aspectos epidemiológicos, clasificación e implicaciones clínicas y de prevención del virus del dengue en el paciente pediátrico. RELEVANCIA: La evidencia sobre la enfermedad del dengue que involucra niños es escasa, con reportes epidemiológicos que datan más de una década; y no describen las implicaciones de las formas graves del virus del dengue en el grupo pediátrico que tienen mayor riesgo de hospitalización por complicaciones relacionadas con alteraciones de la permeabilidad vascular, hemostasia, trombocitopenia entre otras que sugieren daño a órganos diana y choque; es decir, las complejas interacciones entre el virus del dengue, el sistema inmunitario y los factores propios del huésped determinan el nivel de gravedad de la enfermedad en niños y adolescentes con aumento del riesgo de hospitalización y mortalidad.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the lack of updated information on the epidemiological aspects, classification and clinical implications of dengue virus in pediatric patients, given the increasing number of cases reported annually.
Design and population
The study is a review of the current evidence on dengue virus in pediatric patients, focusing on epidemiological data, classification criteria and clinical implications, with an emphasis on the risk factors associated with severe forms of the disease.
Core methods
The study involved a comprehensive review of existing literature on dengue virus, with a focus on pediatric populations, including epidemiological data, classification systems and clinical outcomes related to severe dengue.
Main results
The evidence highlights that dengue disease in children is associated with a higher risk of hospitalization and mortality due to complications such as vascular permeability, hemostasis, thrombocytopenia and organ damage.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the severity of dengue in children is influenced by complex interactions between the virus, the immune system and host factors, which is a novel perspective in understanding pediatric dengue outcomes.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform improved surveillance, diagnosis and prevention strategies for dengue in children, particularly in identifying risk factors for severe disease and guiding clinical management.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on existing literature, which may have limited recent data, and the evidence is considered moderate due to the reliance on observational reports rather than experimental data.

登革热疫苗研究进展:当前及新兴疫苗的全面综述

Progress on Dengue Vaccines: A Comprehensive Review of Current and Emerging Vaccines
作者： Crisanta A Simon, Joel V Chua, Kirsten E Lyke
摘要中文翻译摘要 登革病毒的全球负担持续增加,这主要由环境变化、城市化发展以及埃及伊蚊的广泛传播所驱动。开发一种有效的登革热疫苗仍然具有挑战性,因为其必须提供对四种流行血清型的持久、均衡的免疫力,同时避免在接触不同血清型时引发免疫介导的疾病增强风险。本文综述了当前对登革热免疫发病机制的理解,强调了第一代减毒活疫苗的局限性,并探讨了既往血清型状态、疫苗对各血清型的效力以及安全性考量如何影响疫苗的实施。我们还回顾了新兴疫苗平台,包括定点减毒、异源加强策略以及mRNA、DNA和肽疫苗。抗原工程领域的进展,结合更先进的诊断技术和疫苗技术,将是克服现有免疫学和实施障碍的关键。持续的创新、公平的疫苗获取以及公众信任对于实现有效的全球登革热控制至关重要。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述聚焦于登革病毒及其疾病,具体探讨了开发一种能对所有四种血清型提供持久免疫力且不会导致免疫介导疾病增强的有效疫苗所面临的挑战。核心问题是既往血清型状态、疫苗对各血清型的效力以及安全性考量如何影响疫苗的实施。
证据基础与方法
本综述是对登革热免疫发病机制及疫苗开发的全面分析。它讨论了第一代减毒活疫苗,并探讨了新兴平台,如定点减毒、异源加强策略以及mRNA、DNA和肽疫苗。综述未明确报告元分析或共识过程,但提供了该领域的详细概述。
共识与结论
综述指出,第一代疫苗存在局限性,尤其是在对各血清型提供均衡免疫力和避免疾病增强方面。它还强调了理解免疫发病机制以及既往血清型状态对疫苗效果的重要性。新兴疫苗平台被讨论为解决这些挑战的潜在方案。
争议与缺口
疫苗对各血清型的效力存在异质性,且对免疫介导疾病增强的担忧仍然存在。综述指出,这些问题尚未解决,且在最佳策略方面缺乏共识。此外,综述还指出了对免疫增强机制和疫苗长期安全性的理解存在不足。
科研与实践启示
综述建议,抗原工程领域的进展,结合更先进的诊断技术和疫苗技术,是克服现有免疫学和实施障碍的关键。它还呼吁持续的创新、公平的疫苗获取以及公众信任,以实现有效的全球登革热控制。

Progress on Dengue Vaccines: A Comprehensive Review of Current and Emerging Vaccines

Authors: Crisanta A Simon, Joel V Chua, Kirsten E Lyke
Original abstract (English)
Abstract The global burden of dengue virus continues to increase, driven by environmental changes, urban growth, and the widespread dissemination of Aedes mosquito vectors. Developing an effective dengue vaccine remains challenging because it must provide long-lasting, balanced immunity against each of the four circulating serotypes without increasing the risk of immune-mediated disease enhancement upon exposure to a different serotype. This review article summarizes current understanding of dengue immunopathogenesis, highlights the limitations of first-generation, live-attenuated vaccines, and explores how preexisting serostatus, efficacy across serotypes, and safety considerations affect vaccine implementation. We also review emerging vaccine platforms, including site-directed attenuation, heterologous prime-boost strategies, and mRNA, DNA, and peptide vaccines. Advances in antigen engineering, together with improved diagnostics and surveillance, will be essential to overcoming existing immunologic and implementation barriers. Ongoing innovation, equitable access, and public trust remain critical to achieving effective global dengue control.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on dengue virus and dengue disease, specifically examining the challenges of developing an effective vaccine that provides long-lasting immunity against all four serotypes without causing immune-mediated disease enhancement. The central question is how preexisting serostatus, efficacy across serotypes, and safety considerations influence vaccine implementation.
Evidence base and method
The review is a comprehensive analysis of dengue immunopathogenesis and vaccine development. It includes a discussion of first-generation, live-attenuated vaccines and explores emerging platforms such as site-directed attenuation, heterologous prime-boost strategies, and mRNA, DNA, and peptide vaccines. The review does not explicitly report on meta-analysis or consensus processes, but it provides a detailed overview of the field.
Consensus and conclusions
The review highlights that first-generation vaccines have limitations, particularly in balancing immunity across serotypes and avoiding disease enhancement. It also emphasizes the importance of understanding immunopathogenesis and the role of preexisting serostatus in vaccine effectiveness. Emerging vaccine platforms are discussed as potential solutions to these challenges.
Controversies and gaps
There is heterogeneity in vaccine efficacy across serotypes and concerns about immune-mediated disease enhancement. The review notes that these issues remain unresolved, and there is a lack of consensus on the best strategies to address them. Additionally, the review identifies gaps in understanding the mechanisms of immune enhancement and the long-term safety of vaccines.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that advances in antigen engineering, along with improved diagnostics and surveillance, are essential to overcoming existing immunologic and implementation barriers. It also calls for ongoing innovation, equitable access, and public trust to achieve effective global dengue control.

治疗性血浆置换和连续肾脏替代治疗用于登革热伴多器官衰竭的病例:病例报告与重点综述

Therapeutic plasma exchange and continuous renal replacement therapy for severe dengue with multiorgan failure: a case report and focused review.
作者： Alexandre Mestre Tejo, Maysa Sales Dos Santos, Pedro Fortes Osório Bustamante, Flávia Vanessa Carvalho Sousa Esteves, Felipe Medauar Reis de Andrade Moreira, Ursula Castelo-Branco, Dania Abdel Rahman Pereira, Yeh-Li Ho
摘要中文翻译登革热可进展为急性肝衰竭、溶血、脑病、休克和多器官功能障碍,但治疗性血浆置换在登革热治疗中的作用仍不确定。我们报告了一例17岁青少年登革热病例,其NS1阳性,接受了连续肾脏替代治疗、治疗性血浆置换和全面的重症监护,并提供了治疗性血浆置换在登革热伴多器官衰竭中的重点叙述性综述。该患者出现了急性血管内溶血、严重贫血、血小板减少、凝血功能障碍、需要侵入性机械通气的脑病、少尿性急性肾损伤、休克和急性肝衰竭。她还符合2004年HLH标准中8项中的5项,提示存在继发性噬血细胞性淋巴组织组织细胞增生症。在进行三次治疗性血浆置换之前,开始使用连续肾脏替代治疗,首次置换量为估计血浆体积的1·5倍,使用新鲜冰冻血浆。随后,血流动力学、肝脏、肾脏和凝血参数均有所改善。继发感染和需要视频辅助胸腔镜引流的血胸使康复过程复杂化;患者在入院38天后出院。目前关于治疗性血浆置换在登革热伴多器官衰竭中的应用证据主要来自病例报告、小样本系列研究和观察性队列研究。治疗性血浆置换可被视为某些难治性登革热病例的辅助挽救性治疗,特别是那些表现为急性肝衰竭或血栓性微血管病的病例,但其独立疗效、最佳时机、用药方案和适用人群仍不明确。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
综述聚焦于登革热伴多器官衰竭,特别是探讨治疗性血浆置换作为一种治疗手段的作用。核心问题是治疗性血浆置换是否有效用于管理登革热,尤其是在涉及急性肝衰竭、溶血、脑病、休克和多器官功能障碍的病例中。
证据基础与方法
证据基础包括病例报告和重点叙述性综述。综述未采用系统性或荟萃分析方法,而是依赖于单个病例研究和现有文献。所纳入的研究主要为病例报告、小样本系列和观察性队列研究,没有明确提及荟萃分析或共识过程。
共识与结论
综述得出结论,治疗性血浆置换可被视为某些难治性登革热病例的辅助挽救性治疗,特别是那些表现为急性肝衰竭或血栓性微血管病的病例。然而,该疗法的独立疗效、最佳时机、用药方案和适用人群仍不明确。
争议与缺口
关于治疗性血浆置换在登革热中的应用证据仅限于病例报告和小样本观察性研究,这些可能不足以确立其疗效或最佳使用方式。此外,其在登革热治疗中的作用仍存在不确定性,尚不清楚应作为一线或二线干预措施。
科研与实践启示
需要进一步研究以明确治疗性血浆置换在登革热中的独立疗效、最佳时机、用药方案和适用人群。这包括在特定临床情境下进行更大规模、随机对照试验以评估其有效性与安全性。

Therapeutic plasma exchange and continuous renal replacement therapy for severe dengue with multiorgan failure: a case report and focused review.

Authors: Alexandre Mestre Tejo, Maysa Sales Dos Santos, Pedro Fortes Osório Bustamante, Flávia Vanessa Carvalho Sousa Esteves, Felipe Medauar Reis de Andrade Moreira, Ursula Castelo-Branco, Dania Abdel Rahman Pereira, Yeh-Li Ho
Original abstract (English)
Severe dengue can progress to acute liver failure, haemolysis, encephalopathy, shock, and multiorgan dysfunction, but the role of therapeutic plasma exchange in treatment remains uncertain. We report a 17-year-old adolescent with NS1-positive dengue managed with continuous renal replacement therapy, therapeutic plasma exchange, and comprehensive intensive care, and provide a focused narrative review of therapeutic plasma exchange in severe dengue. The patient developed abrupt massive intravascular haemolysis, profound anaemia, thrombocytopenia, coagulopathy, encephalopathy requiring invasive mechanical ventilation, oliguric acute kidney injury, shock, and acute liver failure. She also met five of eight HLH-2004 criteria for secondary haemophagocytic lymphohistiocytosis. Continuous renal replacement therapy was initiated before three therapeutic plasma exchange sessions, beginning with an exchange of 1·5 estimated plasma volumes using fresh frozen plasma. Haemodynamic, hepatic, renal, and coagulation parameters subsequently improved. Secondary infection and haemothorax requiring video-assisted thoracoscopic drainage complicated recovery; she was discharged 38 days after admission. Published evidence on the use of therapeutic plasma exchange in severe dengue consists mainly of case reports, small series, and observational cohorts. Therapeutic plasma exchange can be considered an adjunctive rescue therapy in selected refractory cases, particularly acute liver failure or thrombotic microangiopathy phenotypes, but its independent benefit, optimal timing, prescription, and target population remain undefined.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on severe dengue with multiorgan failure, specifically examining the role of therapeutic plasma exchange as a treatment modality. The central question is whether therapeutic plasma exchange is effective in managing severe dengue, particularly in cases involving acute liver failure, haemolysis, encephalopathy, shock, and multiorgan dysfunction.
Evidence base and method
The evidence base consists of a case report and a focused narrative review. The review does not include systematic or meta-analytic methods, but rather relies on a single case study and existing literature. The included studies are primarily case reports, small series, and observational cohorts, with no explicit mention of meta-analysis or consensus processes.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that therapeutic plasma exchange can be considered an adjunctive rescue therapy in selected refractory cases of severe dengue, particularly those involving acute liver failure or thrombotic microangiopathy phenotypes. However, the independent benefit, optimal timing, prescription, and target population for this therapy remain undefined.
Controversies and gaps
The evidence on therapeutic plasma exchange for severe dengue is limited to case reports and small observational studies, which may not provide sufficient data to establish its efficacy or optimal use. There is also uncertainty regarding its role in treatment and whether it should be used as a primary or secondary intervention.
Research and practice implications
Further research is needed to define the independent benefit, optimal timing, prescription, and target population for therapeutic plasma exchange in severe dengue. This includes larger, randomized controlled trials to assess its effectiveness and safety in specific clinical scenarios.

疟疾控制中改良昆虫释放干预措施的建模:一项范围综述及与登革热的专题比较

Modelling modified insect release interventions for malaria control: a scoping review and thematic comparison with dengue.
作者： Preethi Lakshminarayanan, Katie Tiley, Ah-Young Lim, Katie Susong, Mojca Kristan, Thomas Walker, Oliver Brady
摘要中文翻译沃尔巴克氏体是一种有前景的新干预措施,可用于疟疾控制。虽然建模支持了沃尔巴克氏体在登革热控制中的部署,但其在按蚊中的应用仍处于起步阶段。我们系统地回顾了使用PubMed和Embase数据库的疟疾中基于沃尔巴克氏体的改良昆虫释放动态传播模型。证据主要与一项平行的沃尔巴克氏体-埃及伊蚊范围综述中的沃尔巴克氏体模型进行比较,次要地与通过独立系统搜索识别出的基于基因驱动的按蚊模型进行比较。在纳入的110项研究中,发现了三个主要的证据缺口。首先,很少有模型纳入了与环境相关的适应性效应,许多模型假设高母体传播(MT)和胞质不相容性(CI),这限制了对环境变化如何影响沃尔巴克氏体持续性的评估。其次,尽管有证据表明蚊子的移动影响了沃尔巴克氏体的传播,但空间异质性很少被建模。第三,很少有研究将沃尔巴克氏体动态嵌入到整合的疟疾传播模型中,这阻碍了对沃尔巴克氏体在疟疾控制中的流行病学影响的估计。从沃尔巴克氏体在登革热控制中的经验以及基因驱动的平行发展,提供了一个已建立的框架,具有可操作的改进路径。解决这些优先事项对于指导沃尔巴克氏体作为新的疟疾控制干预措施的安全、有效和可扩展实施至关重要。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述聚焦于疟疾控制中改良昆虫释放干预措施的建模,特别是探讨基于沃尔巴克氏体的策略。它将这些策略与基于沃尔巴克氏体的登革热模型以及基于基因驱动的按蚊模型进行比较,以评估沃尔巴克氏体在疟疾控制中的潜力。
证据基础与方法
本综述是一项范围综述,系统地检查了使用PubMed和Embase数据库的疟疾中基于沃尔巴克氏体的改良昆虫释放动态传播模型。证据主要与一项平行的沃尔巴克氏体-埃及伊蚊范围综述中的沃尔巴克氏体模型进行比较,次要地与通过独立系统搜索识别出的基于基因驱动的按蚊模型进行比较。
共识与结论
建模支持了沃尔巴克氏体在登革热控制中的部署,类似的框架可能适用于疟疾。然而,沃尔巴克氏体在按蚊中用于疟疾控制的应用仍处于起步阶段,其整合到整合的疟疾传播模型中的程度有限。
争议与缺口
发现了三个主要的证据缺口:与环境相关的适应性效应很少被纳入模型,空间异质性很少被建模,且很少有研究将沃尔巴克氏体动态嵌入到整合的疟疾传播模型中。这些缺口限制了评估环境变化如何影响沃尔巴克氏体持续性及其对疟疾控制的流行病学影响的能力。
科研与实践启示
解决这些缺口对于指导沃尔巴克氏体作为新的疟疾控制干预措施的安全、有效和可扩展实施至关重要。从登革热控制的经验以及基因驱动的平行发展,为推进基于沃尔巴克氏体的疟疾控制策略提供了已建立的框架。

Modelling modified insect release interventions for malaria control: a scoping review and thematic comparison with dengue.

Authors: Preethi Lakshminarayanan, Katie Tiley, Ah-Young Lim, Katie Susong, Mojca Kristan, Thomas Walker, Oliver Brady
Original abstract (English)
Wolbachia is a promising new intervention for malaria control. While modelling has supported Wolbachia deployment for dengue control, its application in Anopheles is still emerging. We systematically reviewed dynamic transmission models of Wolbachia-based modified insect releases for malaria using PubMed and Embase. Evidence was compared primarily with Wolbachia-based dengue models from a parallel Wolbachia-Aedes aegypti scoping review and secondarily with gene drive-based Anopheles models identified through a separate systematic search. Across 110 included studies, three major gaps were identified. First, context-dependent fitness effects are rarely incorporated, with many models assuming high maternal transmission (MT) and cytoplasmic incompatibility (CI), limiting assessment of how environmental variability influences persistence. Second, spatial heterogeneity is infrequently modelled despite evidence that mosquito movement shapes Wolbachia spread. Third, few studies embed Wolbachia dynamics within integrated malaria transmission models, preventing estimation of epidemiological impact to assess Wolbachia's role in malaria control and elimination. Lessons from Wolbachia-based dengue control and parallel developments in gene drive provide an established framework, with actionable pathways for advancement. Addressing these priorities will be essential to guide safe, effective and scalable implementation of Wolbachia as a new malaria control intervention.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on modelling modified insect release interventions for malaria control, specifically examining Wolbachia-based strategies. It compares these with Wolbachia-based dengue models and gene drive-based Anopheles models to assess the potential of Wolbachia for malaria control.
Evidence base and method
The review is a scoping review that systematically examined dynamic transmission models of Wolbachia-based modified insect releases for malaria using PubMed and Embase. Evidence was primarily compared with Wolbachia-based dengue models from a parallel scoping review and secondarily with gene drive-based Anopheles models identified through a separate systematic search.
Consensus and conclusions
Modelling has supported Wolbachia deployment for dengue control, and similar frameworks may be applicable to malaria. However, the application of Wolbachia in Anopheles mosquitoes for malaria remains emerging, with limited integration into integrated malaria transmission models.
Controversies and gaps
Three major gaps were identified: context-dependent fitness effects are rarely incorporated, spatial heterogeneity is infrequently modelled, and few studies embed Wolbachia dynamics within integrated malaria transmission models. These gaps limit the ability to assess how environmental variability influences Wolbachia persistence and its epidemiological impact on malaria control.
Research and practice implications
Addressing these gaps will be essential to guide safe, effective, and scalable implementation of Wolbachia as a new malaria control intervention. Lessons from dengue control and parallel developments in gene drive provide an established framework for advancing Wolbachia-based strategies for malaria.

登革病毒疫苗在儿童和青少年中的应用:现有证据、安全性及未来方向

Dengue Vaccination in Children and Adolescents: Current Evidence, Safety, and Future Directions
作者： Marco Masetti, Giulia Zambelli, Susanna Esposito
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
登革热是一种不断扩展的蚊媒传染病,已成为日益严重的全球健康威胁。其四种血清型、不断变化的病毒特性以及复杂的发病机制使得疫苗研发面临特别挑战。本综述探讨了登革病毒疫苗,特别关注儿童和青少年群体。
证据基础与方法
从数据库创建起至2026年11月10日,检索了PubMed/MEDLINE、Scopus、Web of Science和Cochrane图书馆。评估证据时特别关注儿科人群、年龄、基线血清状态、感染血清型及保护持续时间。
共识与结论
CYD-TDV提供中等程度的保护,但长期随访发现血清阴性受种者住院或重症登革热风险增加,因此在使用前进行血清学筛查至关重要。TAK-003可保护儿童和青少年免受病毒学确诊和住院的登革病毒感染,但其有效性因血清型和血清状态而异;血清阴性受种者对DENV-3和DENV-4的保护效果尚不确定。在五年期间,III期试验中未报告的疫苗相关不良事件发生率,包括严重事件,与安慰剂相当。登革病毒疫苗标志着重要进展,但其益处和风险取决于年龄、血清状态及流行血清型。
争议与缺口
尚无经过验证的保护相关指标。TV003/TV005及其他平台拓宽了疫苗研发管线,但针对儿童的有效性证据仍有限。目前尚未确定因果关系。
科研与实践启示
2025年12月8日,ANVISA注册了疫苗用于12至59岁人群,巴西随后在2026年6月暂停了疫苗接种策略,以调查在接种约500,000剂后出现的42份报告,其中包括3例严重病例和2例死亡。可负担性、公平获取、监测以及护理人员信心仍然是重要的实施挑战。长期来看,需要进行针对血清型的评估以及上市后的监测监测。下一代疫苗、清晰的沟通以及可持续的获取将是实现儿童持久且公平保护的关键。

Dengue Vaccination in Children and Adolescents: Current Evidence, Safety, and Future Directions

Authors: Marco Masetti, Giulia Zambelli, Susanna Esposito
Original abstract (English)
Background: Dengue is an expanding mosquito-borne disease and a growing global health threat. Its four serotypes, changing epidemiology, and complex immune responses make vaccine development particularly challenging. This review examines dengue vaccines, with emphasis on children and adolescents. Methods: PubMed/MEDLINE, Scopus, Web of Science, and the Cochrane Library were searched from inception through 10 November 2026. Clinical trials, observational studies, reviews, surveillance reports, and guidelines addressing dengue immunopathogenesis, vaccine development, efficacy, immunogenicity, safety, effectiveness, or implementation were considered. Evidence was assessed with attention to pediatric populations, age, baseline serostatus, infecting serotype, and duration of protection. Results: CYD-TDV provides moderate protection, but long-term follow-up identified an increased risk of hospitalized or severe dengue in seronegative recipients, making pre-vaccination screening central to its use. TAK-003 protects children and adolescents against virologically confirmed and hospitalized dengue, although efficacy varies by serotype and serostatus; protection against DENV-3 and DENV-4 in seronegative recipients remains uncertain. Butantan-DV showed efficacy across pediatric age groups and baseline serostatus, although the absence of DENV-3 and DENV-4 cases precluded serotype-specific estimates. Over five years, phase III rates of unsolicited vaccine-related adverse events, including serious events, were comparable with placebo. After ANVISA registered the vaccine for ages 12–59 on 8 December 2025, Brazil paused its vaccination strategy in June 2026 while investigating 42 reports following approximately 500,000 doses, including three severe cases and two deaths. Causality has not been established. TV003/TV005 and other platforms broaden the pipeline, though pediatric efficacy evidence remains limited. No validated correlate of protection is available. Affordability, equitable access, surveillance, and caregiver confidence remain important implementation challenges. Conclusions: Dengue vaccines mark important progress, but their benefits and risks depend on age, serostatus, and circulating serotypes. Long-term, serotype-specific evaluation and post-marketing surveillance are needed. Next-generation vaccines, clear communication, and sustainable access will be essential to achieve durable, equitable protection for children.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Dengue is an expanding mosquito-borne disease and a growing global health threat. Its four serotypes, changing epidemiology, and complex immune responses make vaccine development particularly challenging. This review examines dengue vaccines, with emphasis on children and adolescents.
Evidence base and method
PubMed/MEDLINE, Scopus, Web of Science, and the Cochrane Library were searched from inception through 10 November 2026. Evidence was assessed with attention to pediatric populations, age, baseline serostatus, infecting serotype, and duration of protection.
Consensus and conclusions
CYD-TDV provides moderate protection, but long-term follow-up identified an increased risk of hospitalized or severe dengue in seronegative recipients, making pre-vaccination screening central to its use. TAK-003 protects children and adolescents against virologically confirmed and hospitalized dengue, although efficacy varies by serotype and serostatus; protection against DENV-3 and DENV-4 in seronegative recipients remains uncertain. Over five years, phase III rates of unsolicited vaccine-related adverse events, including serious events, were comparable with placebo. Dengue vaccines mark important progress, but their benefits and risks depend on age, serostatus, and circulating serotypes.
Controversies and gaps
No validated correlate of protection is available. TV003/TV005 and other platforms broaden the pipeline, though pediatric efficacy evidence remains limited. Causality has not been established.
Research and practice implications
After ANVISA registered the vaccine for ages 12–59 on 8 December 2025, Brazil paused its vaccination strategy in June 2026 while investigating 42 reports following approximately 500,000 doses, including three severe cases and two deaths. Affordability, equitable access, surveillance, and caregiver confidence remain important implementation challenges. Long-term, serotype-specific evaluation and post-marketing surveillance are needed. Next-generation vaccines, clear communication, and sustainable access will be essential to achieve durable, equitable protection for children.
